/**
 * ACE Campo API - producao ajustada
 * API limpa para Google Apps Script / Google Sheets.
 * Sem simulacao, sem base sintetica e sem fluxo antigo de cartao.
 *
 * Ajustes aplicados:
 * - Pacote 1 de seguranca critica incorporado.
 * - Bootstrap compativel com app atual: envia senhaHash somente quando auth=1.
 * - Headers de visitas sem card_code e sem card_virtual_url.
 * - Login por POST disponivel.
 * - Sync protegido por token em Script Properties e LockService.
 */

/**
 * ACE Campo - Pacote 1 de Seguranca Critica
 *
 * Objetivos:
 * - Remover dependencia da senha padrao "adm".
 * - Guardar SPREADSHEET_ID fora do GitHub.
 * - Guardar API_TOKEN fora do GitHub.
 * - Gerar senha administrativa temporaria forte.
 * - Corrigir administrador ADM existente se ele ainda usa a senha padrao.
 * - Impedir que a aba Config guarde segredos reais.
 */

;

/**
 * Helpers defensivos para Google Apps Script.
 * Evitam "Exception: Argumento inválido: key" quando alguma chave chega vazia,
 * inválida ou quando o projeto está sendo migrado para outra planilha.
 */
function ACE_safePropertyKey_(key) {
  key = String(key || '').trim();
  if (!key) { return ''; }
  return key.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]/g, '_').slice(0, 250);
}

function ACE_getScriptProperty_(key, fallback) {
  key = ACE_safePropertyKey_(key);
  if (!key) { return fallback || ''; }

  try {
    var value = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(key);
    if (value === null || value === undefined) { return fallback || ''; }
    return value;
  } catch (err) {
    return fallback || '';
  }
}

function ACE_setScriptProperty_(key, value) {
  key = ACE_safePropertyKey_(key);
  if (!key) { return false; }

  try {
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(
      key,
      String(value === null || value === undefined ? '' : value)
    );
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    return false;
  }
}

function ACE_deleteScriptProperty_(key) {
  key = ACE_safePropertyKey_(key);
  if (!key) { return false; }

  try {
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(key);
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    return false;
  }
}

function ACE_safeCacheKey_(key) {
  key = String(key || '').trim().replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]/g, '_');
  if (!key) { return 'ACE_EMPTY_KEY'; }
  return key.slice(0, 240);
}

function ACE_cacheGet_(key) {
  try {
    return CacheService.getScriptCache().get(ACE_safeCacheKey_(key));
  } catch (err) {
    return null;
  }
}

function ACE_cachePut_(key, value, seconds) {
  try {
    CacheService.getScriptCache().put(
      ACE_safeCacheKey_(key),
      String(value === null || value === undefined ? '' : value),
      Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(seconds || 300) || 300, 21600))
    );
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    return false;
  }
}

function ACE_cacheRemove_(key) {
  try {
    CacheService.getScriptCache().remove(ACE_safeCacheKey_(key));
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    return false;
  }
}

function getTargetSpreadsheet_() {
  var secureId = String(ACE_getScriptProperty_(ACE_SECURITY.PROP_SPREADSHEET_ID, '') || '').trim();
  var legacyId = String(ACE_getScriptProperty_('SPREADSHEET_ID', '') || '').trim();

  if (secureId) {
    return SpreadsheetApp.openById(secureId);
  }

  if (legacyId) {
    return SpreadsheetApp.openById(legacyId);
  }

  var active = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  if (active) {
    return active;
  }

  throw new Error(
    'Planilha nao configurada. Configure ' +
    ACE_SECURITY.PROP_SPREADSHEET_ID + ' em Script Properties ou execute o menu "Definir ESTA planilha como banco ativo".'
  );
}

function normalizeComparableText_(value) {
  var normalized = String(value || '')
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[’´`']/g, ' ')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9/]+/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();

  if (normalized === 'caixa d agua') {
    return ACE_CANONICAL_TERRITORY.BAIRRO_KEY;
  }
  return normalized;
}

function stripMicroareaPrefix_(value) {
  return String(value || '').replace(/^[A-Z0-9]{1,8}\s*-\s*/i, '').trim();
}

function normalizeBairroValue_(value) {
  var raw = String(value || '').trim();
  var key = normalizeComparableText_(raw);
  var strippedKey = normalizeComparableText_(stripMicroareaPrefix_(raw));

  if (key === ACE_CANONICAL_TERRITORY.BAIRRO_KEY || strippedKey === ACE_CANONICAL_TERRITORY.BAIRRO_KEY) {
    return ACE_CANONICAL_TERRITORY.BAIRRO_LABEL;
  }
  return raw;
}

function normalizeMicroareaValue_(value, bairro) {
  var raw = String(value || '').trim();
  var key = normalizeComparableText_(raw);
  var strippedKey = normalizeComparableText_(stripMicroareaPrefix_(raw));

  if (
    /^M04\b/i.test(raw) ||
    key === ACE_CANONICAL_TERRITORY.BAIRRO_KEY ||
    strippedKey === ACE_CANONICAL_TERRITORY.BAIRRO_KEY
  ) {
    return ACE_CANONICAL_TERRITORY.MICROAREA_LABEL;
  }

  // Bairro e microárea são campos administrativos independentes.
  // Não deduzir microárea a partir do bairro.
  return raw;
}

function normalizeDelimitedMicroareas_(value) {
  return String(value || '')
    .split('|')
    .map(function(item) { return normalizeMicroareaValue_(item, ''); })
    .map(function(item) { return String(item || '').trim(); })
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join('|');
}

function normalizeTerritoryRowByHeaders_(row, headers) {
  var clean = Object.assign({}, row || {});
  var hasHeader = function(name) { return (headers || []).indexOf(name) > -1; };

  if (hasHeader('nome')) {
    clean.nome = normalizeBairroValue_(clean.nome);
  }
  if (hasHeader('bairro_base')) {
    clean.bairro_base = normalizeBairroValue_(clean.bairro_base);
  }
  if (hasHeader('bairro')) {
    clean.bairro = normalizeBairroValue_(clean.bairro);
  }
  if (hasHeader('microareas_sugeridas')) {
    clean.microareas_sugeridas = normalizeDelimitedMicroareas_(clean.microareas_sugeridas);
  }
  if (hasHeader('base_microarea')) {
    clean.base_microarea = normalizeMicroareaValue_(clean.base_microarea, clean.base_region || '');
  }
  if (hasHeader('microarea')) {
    clean.microarea = normalizeMicroareaValue_(clean.microarea, clean.bairro || '');
  }
  if (hasHeader('gps_territory')) {
    clean.gps_territory = normalizeBairroValue_(clean.gps_territory);
  }
  return clean;
}

function onOpen() {
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi()
    .createMenu('ACE Sistema')
    .addItem('Preparar estrutura da planilha', 'setupProductionSheets')
    .addItem('Reparar login ADM por CPF', 'repairAdminCpfLogin_')
    .addItem('Criar administrador inicial', 'createInitialAdmin')
    .addItem('FORÇAR ADM 111 / ASDfg123', 'ACE_FORCAR_ADM_INICIAL_111')
    .addItem('Recalcular metricas de hoje', 'rebuildTodayMetrics')
    .addItem("Migrar e padronizar Caixa d'Água", 'migrateCanonicalCaixaDAguaData_')
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem('Resetar dados operacionais', 'resetOperationalData_')
    .addItem('PERIGO: apagar agentes e recriar ADM', 'resetAgentsAndRecreateAdmin_')
    .addItem('PERIGO: reset completo preservando bairros/ruas/config', 'resetCleanBasePreservingReferenceData_')
    .addItem('Gerar demo 200 imoveis / 300 visitas (jan-mar/2026)', 'generatePresentationDemoData_')
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem('Diagnosticar banco ativo', 'diagnosticarBancoAtivo_')
    .addItem('Definir ESTA planilha como banco ativo', 'ACE_useActiveSpreadsheetAsDatabase_')
    .addItem('Mostrar credencial ADM ativa', 'ACE_showActiveAdminCredentials_')
    .addItem('Limpar ponteiro ACE_SPREADSHEET_ID', 'ACE_clearDatabasePointer_')
    .addItem('Limpar log operacional', 'clearOperationalLog')
    .addToUi();
}

function setupProductionSheets() {
  var ss = getTargetSpreadsheet_();
  Object.keys(ACE_API.HEADERS).forEach(function(sheetName) {
    ensureSheetWithHeaders_(ss, sheetName, ACE_API.HEADERS[sheetName]);
  });
  seedConfigDefaults_();
  seedBairrosCatalog_();
  seedSystemDefaults_();
  ensureDefaultAdminForCpfLogin_();
  markProductionSetupReady_();
  getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Estrutura pronta para uso.', 'ACE Campo', 5);
  return { ok: true, message: 'Estrutura pronta para uso.' };
}

function getProductionSetupCacheKey_() {
  return 'ACE_SETUP_READY_' + ACE_API.VERSION;
}

function markProductionSetupReady_() {
  ACE_cachePut_(getProductionSetupCacheKey_(), '1', 21600);
}

function ensureProductionSheetsCached_() {
  try {
    if (ACE_cacheGet_(getProductionSetupCacheKey_()) === '1') {
      return;
    }
    setupProductionSheets();
    ACE_cachePut_(getProductionSetupCacheKey_(), '1', 21600);
  } catch (err) {
    setupProductionSheets();
  }
}

function getAdminInitialPassword_(forceNew) {
  var password = ACE_SECURITY.INITIAL_ADMIN_PASSWORD || 'ASDfg123';
  ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_ADMIN_INITIAL_PASSWORD', password);
  return password;
}

function generateTemporaryAdminCpf_() {
  return ACE_SECURITY.INITIAL_ADMIN_CPF || '11111111111';
}

function getAdminInitialCpf_(forceNew) {
  var cpf = normalizeCpfValue_(ACE_SECURITY.INITIAL_ADMIN_CPF || '11111111111');
  ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_ADMIN_INITIAL_CPF', cpf);
  return cpf;
}

function generateTemporaryAdminPassword_() {
  var upper = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ';
  var lower = 'abcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz';
  var digits = '23456789';
  var symbols = '@#$%*-_+=!';
  var all = upper + lower + digits + symbols;
  var chars = [
    pickTemporaryPasswordChar_(upper),
    pickTemporaryPasswordChar_(lower),
    pickTemporaryPasswordChar_(digits),
    pickTemporaryPasswordChar_(symbols)
  ];

  while (chars.length < 18) {
    chars.push(pickTemporaryPasswordChar_(all));
  }

  return shuffleTemporaryPasswordChars_(chars).join('');
}

function pickTemporaryPasswordChar_(chars) {
  return chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
}

function shuffleTemporaryPasswordChars_(items) {
  var copy = items.slice();
  for (var i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i -= 1) {
    var j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    var temp = copy[i];
    copy[i] = copy[j];
    copy[j] = temp;
  }
  return copy;
}

function ensureDefaultAdminForCpfLogin_() {
  var rows = buildVisibleAgentRoster_();
  var now = nowIso_();
  var admin = rows.find(function(row) { return isActiveAdminRow_(row); });
  var fixedApplied = ACE_getScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_FIXED_APPLIED', '') === '1';
  var packageResetApplied = ACE_getScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_111_RESET_V5', '') === '1';
  var initialCpf = getAdminInitialCpf_(false);
  var initialPassword = getAdminInitialPassword_(false);
  var initialHash = sha256Hex_(initialPassword);

  if (admin) {
    var changed = false;
    var currentCpf = normalizeCpfValue_(admin.cpf || '');
    var currentHash = String(admin.senhaHash || '').trim();
    var isDefaultAdminRow = String(admin.uid || '').trim() === ACE_API.ADMIN_UID || String(admin.matricula || '').trim().toUpperCase() === String(ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA || 'ADM').toUpperCase();

    if (!packageResetApplied && (isDefaultAdminRow || currentCpf === initialCpf || normalizeRole_(admin.role || '') === 'administrador')) {
      admin.cpf = initialCpf;
      admin.senhaHash = initialHash;
      ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_FIXED_APPLIED', '1');
      ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_111_RESET_V5', '1');
      changed = true;
    } else if (!fixedApplied && isDefaultAdminRow) {
      admin.cpf = initialCpf;
      admin.senhaHash = initialHash;
      ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_FIXED_APPLIED', '1');
      changed = true;
    } else {
      if (!currentCpf || currentCpf === '00000000000') {
        admin.cpf = initialCpf;
        changed = true;
      }
      if (!currentHash || currentHash === sha256Hex_('adm123') || currentHash === sha256Hex_('admin123')) {
        admin.senhaHash = initialHash;
        changed = true;
      }
    }

    admin.uid = String(admin.uid || ACE_API.ADMIN_UID).trim() || ACE_API.ADMIN_UID;
    admin.nome = normalizeText_(admin.nome || ACE_API.ADMIN_NOME) || ACE_API.ADMIN_NOME;
    admin.matricula = String(admin.matricula || ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA).trim() || ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA;
    admin.role = 'Administrador';
    if (changed) {
      admin.updatedAt = now;
      upsertRows_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, [sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(admin, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes)], 'uid', ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
    }
    return admin;
  }

  admin = {
    uid: ACE_API.ADMIN_UID,
    nome: ACE_API.ADMIN_NOME,
    matricula: ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA,
    cpf: initialCpf,
    role: 'Administrador',
    base_microarea: '',
    base_region: 'Configuracao do sistema',
    senhaHash: initialHash,
    updatedAt: now
  };
  ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_FIXED_APPLIED', '1');
  ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_111_RESET_V5', '1');
  upsertRows_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, [sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(admin, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes)], 'uid', ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  appendLogSafe_('admin', 'ensure_default_cpf_login', admin.uid, 'Administrador inicial criado/conferido. Troque a senha no primeiro acesso.');
  return admin;
}

function createInitialAdmin() {
  setupProductionSheets();
  var rows = buildVisibleAgentRoster_();
  var existingAdmin = rows.find(function(row) {
    return isActiveAdminRow_(row);
  });

  if (existingAdmin) {
    var password = getAdminInitialPassword_(false);
    existingAdmin.uid = String(existingAdmin.uid || ACE_API.ADMIN_UID).trim() || ACE_API.ADMIN_UID;
    existingAdmin.nome = normalizeText_(existingAdmin.nome || ACE_API.ADMIN_NOME) || ACE_API.ADMIN_NOME;
    existingAdmin.matricula = String(existingAdmin.matricula || ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA).trim() || ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA;
    existingAdmin.cpf = getAdminInitialCpf_(false);
    existingAdmin.role = 'Administrador';
    existingAdmin.base_region = normalizeText_(existingAdmin.base_region || 'Configuracao do sistema');
    existingAdmin.senhaHash = sha256Hex_(password);
    existingAdmin.updatedAt = nowIso_();
    ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_FIXED_APPLIED', '1');
    upsertRows_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, [sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(existingAdmin, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes)], 'uid', ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
    appendLogSafe_('admin', 'repair_from_create_initial_admin', existingAdmin.uid || existingAdmin.matricula || 'ADM', 'Administrador inicial recriado/conferido com CPF e senha padrao do pacote.');
    getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Administrador pronto. CPF: ' + existingAdmin.cpf + ' | senha temporaria: ' + password, 'ACE Campo', 10);
    return { ok: true, created: false, repaired: true, admin: buildPublicAgent_(existingAdmin), temporaryPassword: password };
  }

  var password = getAdminInitialPassword_(false);
  var now = nowIso_();
  var admin = {
    uid: ACE_API.ADMIN_UID,
    nome: ACE_API.ADMIN_NOME,
    matricula: ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA,
    cpf: getAdminInitialCpf_(false),
    role: 'Administrador',
    base_microarea: '',
    base_region: 'Configuracao do sistema',
    senhaHash: sha256Hex_(password),
    updatedAt: now
  };

  ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_FIXED_APPLIED', '1');
  upsertRows_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, [sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(admin, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes)], 'uid', ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  appendLogSafe_('admin', 'create', admin.uid, 'Administrador inicial criado.');
  getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Administrador inicial criado. CPF: ' + admin.cpf + ' | senha temporaria: ' + password, 'ACE Campo', 10);
  return { ok: true, created: true, admin: buildPublicAgent_(admin), temporaryPassword: password };
}

function repairAdminCpfLogin_() {
  setupProductionSheets();

  var rows = buildVisibleAgentRoster_();
  var admin = rows.find(function(row) {
    return isActiveAdminRow_(row);
  }) || {};
  var now = nowIso_();
  var password = getAdminInitialPassword_(true);
  var repaired = {
    uid: String(admin.uid || ACE_API.ADMIN_UID).trim() || ACE_API.ADMIN_UID,
    nome: normalizeText_(admin.nome || ACE_API.ADMIN_NOME) || ACE_API.ADMIN_NOME,
    matricula: String(admin.matricula || ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA).trim() || ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA,
    cpf: getAdminInitialCpf_(false),
    role: 'Administrador',
    base_microarea: normalizeText_(admin.base_microarea || ''),
    base_region: normalizeText_(admin.base_region || 'Configuracao do sistema'),
    senhaHash: sha256Hex_(password),
    updatedAt: now
  };

  upsertRows_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, [sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(repaired, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes)], 'uid', ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  appendLogSafe_('admin', 'repair_admin_cpf_login', repaired.uid, 'Login ADM por CPF reparado sem apagar agentes.');
  getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Login ADM reparado. CPF: ' + repaired.cpf + ' | senha temporaria: ' + password, 'ACE Campo', 10);

  return {
    ok: true,
    repaired: true,
    cpf: repaired.cpf,
    temporaryPassword: password,
    admin: buildPublicAgent_(repaired)
  };
}


function ACE_FORCAR_ADM_INICIAL_111() {
  var ss = getTargetSpreadsheet_();
  ensureSheetWithHeaders_(ss, ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  var rows = buildVisibleAgentRoster_();
  var password = ACE_SECURITY.INITIAL_ADMIN_PASSWORD || 'ASDfg123';
  var cpf = normalizeCpfValue_(ACE_SECURITY.INITIAL_ADMIN_CPF || '11111111111');
  var now = nowIso_();
  var admin = rows.find(function(row) {
    return isActiveAdminRow_(row) ||
      String(row.uid || '').trim() === ACE_API.ADMIN_UID ||
      String(row.matricula || '').trim().toUpperCase() === String(ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA || 'ADM').toUpperCase() ||
      normalizeCpfValue_(row.cpf || '') === cpf;
  }) || {};

  admin.uid = String(admin.uid || ACE_API.ADMIN_UID).trim() || ACE_API.ADMIN_UID;
  admin.nome = normalizeText_(admin.nome || ACE_API.ADMIN_NOME) || ACE_API.ADMIN_NOME;
  admin.matricula = String(admin.matricula || ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA).trim() || ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA;
  admin.cpf = cpf;
  admin.role = 'Administrador';
  admin.base_microarea = normalizeText_(admin.base_microarea || '');
  admin.base_region = normalizeText_(admin.base_region || 'Configuracao do sistema');
  admin.senhaHash = sha256Hex_(password);
  admin.updatedAt = now;

  ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_ADMIN_INITIAL_CPF', cpf);
  ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_ADMIN_INITIAL_PASSWORD', password);
  ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_FIXED_APPLIED', '1');
  ACE_setScriptProperty_('ACE_INITIAL_ADMIN_111_RESET_V5', '1');

  upsertRows_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, [sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(admin, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes)], 'uid', ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  appendLogSafe_('admin', 'force_initial_admin_111', admin.uid, 'Administrador inicial forçado para CPF 11111111111 e senha temporaria ASDfg123.');
  ss.toast('ADM forçado. CPF: 111.111.111-11 | senha: ASDfg123', 'ACE Campo', 10);

  return {
    ok: true,
    cpf: '11111111111',
    cpfMask: '111.111.111-11',
    temporaryPassword: password,
    senhaHash: admin.senhaHash
  };
}

function forceInitialAdmin111() {
  return ACE_FORCAR_ADM_INICIAL_111();
}


function rebuildTodayMetrics() {
  setupProductionSheets();
  var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone() || 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  var metrics = buildMetricsFromVisitsByDate_(today);
  if (!metrics) {
    getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Nenhuma visita encontrada para hoje.', 'ACE Campo', 5);
    return { ok: true, updated: false };
  }
  upsertMetricsDaily_(metrics);
  getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Metricas do dia recalculadas.', 'ACE Campo', 5);
  return { ok: true, updated: true, reference_date: today };
}

function clearOperationalLog() {
  setupProductionSheets();
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.LOG, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Log);
  getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Log limpo.', 'ACE Campo', 5);
  return { ok: true, cleared: true };
}

function resetOperationalData_() {
  setupProductionSheets();

  var countsBefore = getCounts_();

  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Imoveis);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Visitas);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.TUBITOS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Tubitos);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OPERATIONS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Operations);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OP_ASSIGNMENTS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.OperationAssignments);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.METRICS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.MetricsDaily);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SUPERVISION, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.SupervisionRequests);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.LOG, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Log);

  try {
    setSystemState_('last_sync_at', '');
    setSystemState_('last_sync_error', '');
    setSystemState_('last_bootstrap_at', '');
  } catch (ignore) {}

  getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Reset operacional concluido.', 'ACE Campo', 6);

  return {
    ok: true,
    reset: true,
    preserved: ['Config', 'BairrosCatalogo', 'RuasBase', 'Agentes', 'SystemState'],
    cleared: ['Imoveis', 'Visitas', 'Tubitos', 'Operations', 'OperationAssignments', 'MetricsDaily', 'Log'],
    counts_before: countsBefore,
    counts_after: getCounts_()
  };
}

function resetAgentsAndRecreateAdmin_() {
  setupProductionSheets();

  var countsBefore = getCounts_();
  var password = getAdminInitialPassword_(true);

  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.LOG, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Log);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SYSTEM, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.SystemState);
  seedSystemDefaults_();

  var created = createInitialAdmin();

  appendLogSafe_('maintenance', 'reset_access', 'SYSTEM', 'Reset de agentes executado. Administrador recriado.');
  getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Agentes limpos e administrador recriado. CPF: ' + ACE_API.ADMIN_CPF + ' | senha: ' + password, 'ACE Campo', 10);

  return {
    ok: true,
    reset: true,
    temporaryPassword: password,
    preserved: ['Config', 'BairrosCatalogo', 'RuasBase', 'Imoveis', 'Visitas', 'MetricsDaily'],
    cleared: ['Agentes', 'Log', 'SystemState'],
    counts_before: countsBefore,
    counts_after: getCounts_(),
    admin: created && created.admin ? created.admin : null
  };
}

function resetCleanBasePreservingReferenceData_() {
  setupProductionSheets();

  var countsBefore = getCounts_();
  var password = getAdminInitialPassword_(true);

  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Imoveis);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Visitas);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.TUBITOS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Tubitos);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OPERATIONS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Operations);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OP_ASSIGNMENTS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.OperationAssignments);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.METRICS, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.MetricsDaily);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SUPERVISION, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.SupervisionRequests);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.LOG, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Log);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SYSTEM, [], ACE_API.HEADERS.SystemState);
  seedSystemDefaults_();
  seedBairrosCatalog_();

  var created = createInitialAdmin();
  var adminCpf = created && created.admin ? normalizeCpfValue_(created.admin.cpf || '') : getAdminInitialCpf_(false);

  appendLogSafe_('maintenance', 'reset_full_clean', 'SYSTEM', 'Reset completo executado preservando configuracoes, bairros e ruas.');
  getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Base limpa concluida. Administrador recriado. CPF: ' + adminCpf + ' | senha: ' + password, 'ACE Campo', 10);

  return {
    ok: true,
    reset: true,
    adminCpf: adminCpf,
    temporaryPassword: password,
    preserved: ['Config', 'BairrosCatalogo', 'RuasBase'],
    cleared: ['Agentes', 'Imoveis', 'Visitas', 'Tubitos', 'MetricsDaily', 'Log', 'SystemState'],
    counts_before: countsBefore,
    counts_after: getCounts_(),
    admin: created && created.admin ? created.admin : null
  };
}

function diagnosticarBancoAtivo_() {
  var ss = getTargetSpreadsheet_();
  var info = {
    ok: true,
    id: ss.getId(),
    nome: ss.getName(),
    url: ss.getUrl(),
    version: ACE_API.VERSION,
    counts: getCounts_()
  };

  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
      'Banco ativo:\n\n' +
      'Nome: ' + info.nome + '\n' +
      'ID: ' + info.id + '\n' +
      'URL: ' + info.url + '\n\n' +
      'Agentes: ' + info.counts.agentes + '\n' +
      'Imoveis: ' + info.counts.imoveis + '\n' +
      'Visitas: ' + info.counts.visitas
    );
  } catch (ignore) {}

  return info;
}


function ACE_useActiveSpreadsheetAsDatabase_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  if (!ss) {
    throw new Error('Nenhuma planilha ativa encontrada. Abra esta função pela planilha que será o banco.');
  }

  ACE_setScriptProperty_(ACE_SECURITY.PROP_SPREADSHEET_ID, ss.getId());
  setupProductionSheets();

  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
      'Banco ativo atualizado.\n\n' +
      'Nome: ' + ss.getName() + '\n' +
      'ID: ' + ss.getId() + '\n\n' +
      'Agora execute "Preparar estrutura da planilha" ou "Reset completo".'
    );
  } catch (ignore) {}

  return { ok: true, spreadsheetId: ss.getId(), spreadsheetUrl: ss.getUrl() };
}

function ACE_clearDatabasePointer_() {
  ACE_deleteScriptProperty_(ACE_SECURITY.PROP_SPREADSHEET_ID);
  ACE_deleteScriptProperty_('SPREADSHEET_ID');

  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
      'Ponteiro ACE_SPREADSHEET_ID limpo.\n\n' +
      'A API passará a usar a planilha ativa quando o código estiver vinculado a ela.'
    );
  } catch (ignore) {}

  return { ok: true, cleared: ['ACE_SPREADSHEET_ID', 'SPREADSHEET_ID'] };
}

function ACE_showActiveAdminCredentials_() {
  setupProductionSheets();

  var rows = buildVisibleAgentRoster_();
  var admin = rows.find(function(row) { return isActiveAdminRow_(row); });
  if (!admin) {
    createInitialAdmin();
    rows = buildVisibleAgentRoster_();
    admin = rows.find(function(row) { return isActiveAdminRow_(row); });
  }

  var cpf = admin ? normalizeCpfValue_(admin.cpf || '') : getAdminInitialCpf_(false);
  var password = getAdminInitialPassword_(false);
  var message =
    'Credencial ADM ativa:\n\n' +
    'CPF: ' + cpf + '\n' +
    'Senha temporária: ' + password + '\n\n' +
    'Troque essa senha depois do primeiro login.';

  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(message);
  } catch (ignore) {}

  return {
    ok: true,
    cpf: cpf,
    temporaryPassword: password,
    admin: admin ? buildPublicAgent_(admin) : null
  };
}



function generatePresentationDemoData_() {
  setupProductionSheets();

  var settings = Object.assign({}, ACE_DEMO_SETTINGS);
  var lockSummary = resetOperationalData_();
  var rng = createSeededRandom_(settings.seed);
  var agents = buildVisibleAgentRoster_().filter(function(agent) {
    return normalizeRole_(agent.role) !== 'administrador';
  });
  if (!agents.length) {
    agents = buildVisibleAgentRoster_();
  }
  if (!agents.length) {
    throw new Error('Cadastre pelo menos um agente antes de gerar a demo.');
  }

  var zoneCatalog = buildDemoZoneCatalog_();
  if (!zoneCatalog.length) {
    throw new Error('Nao foi possivel montar o catalogo territorial da demo.');
  }

  var ruaRows = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.RUAS);
  var demoProperties = buildPresentationDemoProperties_(settings, zoneCatalog, ruaRows, rng);
  var demoVisits = buildPresentationDemoVisits_(settings, demoProperties, agents, rng);
  var metricsRows = buildMetricsRowsForDemoVisits_(demoVisits);

  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS, sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(demoProperties, ACE_API.HEADERS.Imoveis), ACE_API.HEADERS.Imoveis);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS, sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(demoVisits, ACE_API.HEADERS.Visitas), ACE_API.HEADERS.Visitas);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.METRICS, sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(metricsRows, ACE_API.HEADERS.MetricsDaily), ACE_API.HEADERS.MetricsDaily);

  setSystemState_('last_demo_generation_at', nowIso_());
  setSystemState_('last_demo_generation_profile', JSON.stringify({
    propertyCount: settings.propertyCount,
    visitCount: settings.visitCount,
    startDate: settings.startDate,
    endDate: settings.endDate,
    depositRate: settings.depositRate,
    focusRate: settings.focusRate,
    agents: agents.length,
    zones: zoneCatalog.length
  }));

  appendLogSafe_(
    'demo',
    'generate_presentation_dataset',
    'DEMO',
    'Base demo gerada com ' + settings.propertyCount + ' imoveis e ' + settings.visitCount + ' visitas entre ' + settings.startDate + ' e ' + settings.endDate + '.'
  );

  getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast('Demo pronta: 200 imoveis e 300 visitas ficticias.', 'ACE Campo', 8);

  return {
    ok: true,
    generated: true,
    settings: settings,
    reset_before_generation: lockSummary,
    counts_after: getCounts_(),
    agents_used: agents.length,
    zones_used: zoneCatalog.length,
    note: 'Demo distribuida por microareas/quarteiroes com GPS de referencia derivado do arquivo territorial KMZ/KML.'
  };
}

function buildDemoZoneCatalog_() {
  var zones = [];
  var seen = {};
  var withQuarterByTerritory = {};

  Object.keys(ACE_DEMO_KML_QUARTEIRAO_CENTROIDS || {}).forEach(function(key) {
    var parts = String(key || '').split('|');
    var territoryName = parts.shift() || '';
    var quarteirao = parts.join('|');
    var centroid = ACE_DEMO_KML_QUARTEIRAO_CENTROIDS[key] || [];
    var zone = createDemoZone_(territoryName, quarteirao, centroid[0], centroid[1]);
    if (!zone || seen[zone.zoneKey]) { return; }
    seen[zone.zoneKey] = true;
    withQuarterByTerritory[zone.territoryKey] = true;
    zones.push(zone);
  });

  Object.keys(ACE_DEMO_KML_TERRITORY_CENTROIDS || {}).forEach(function(territoryName) {
    var territoryKey = normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(territoryName);
    if (withQuarterByTerritory[territoryKey]) { return; }
    var centroid = ACE_DEMO_KML_TERRITORY_CENTROIDS[territoryName] || [];
    var zone = createDemoZone_(territoryName, '', centroid[0], centroid[1]);
    if (!zone || seen[zone.zoneKey]) { return; }
    seen[zone.zoneKey] = true;
    zones.push(zone);
  });

  if (!zones.length) {
    throw new Error('Catalogo territorial demo vazio.');
  }

  return zones.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.microarea !== b.microarea) {
      return String(a.microarea || '').localeCompare(String(b.microarea || ''), 'pt-BR', { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
    }
    return String(a.quarteirao || '').localeCompare(String(b.quarteirao || ''), 'pt-BR', { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
  });
}

function createDemoZone_(territoryName, quarteirao, lat, lng) {
  var territoryKey = normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(territoryName || '');
  if (!territoryKey) { return null; }
  var bairro = getDemoBairroLabelFromTerritory_(territoryName || '');
  var microarea = getDemoMicroareaLabelFromTerritory_(territoryName || '');
  if (!bairro || !microarea) { return null; }
  return {
    territoryName: String(territoryName || '').trim(),
    territoryKey: territoryKey,
    bairro: bairro,
    microarea: microarea,
    quarteirao: String(quarteirao || '').trim(),
    lat: toNumber_(lat),
    lng: toNumber_(lng),
    zoneKey: territoryKey + '|' + String(quarteirao || '').trim()
  };
}

function buildPresentationDemoProperties_(settings, zoneCatalog, ruaRows, rng) {
  var shuffledZones = shuffleCopy_(zoneCatalog, rng);
  var properties = [];
  var nameSeed = [
    'Ana', 'Carlos', 'Maria', 'Joao', 'Paula', 'Rita', 'Jose', 'Luiza', 'Marcos', 'Aline',
    'Claudia', 'Renato', 'Fabiana', 'Douglas', 'Rosana', 'Leandro', 'Patricia', 'Marta', 'Diego', 'Juliana'
  ];
  var surnameSeed = [
    'Silva', 'Souza', 'Oliveira', 'Santos', 'Costa', 'Pereira', 'Rodrigues', 'Lemgruber', 'Ferreira', 'Gomes',
    'Barbosa', 'Almeida', 'Carvalho', 'Araujo', 'Ramos', 'Machado', 'Campos', 'Teixeira', 'Moraes', 'Martins'
  ];

  var perStreetCounter = {};
  var zoneStreetCursor = {};

  for (var i = 0; i < settings.propertyCount; i += 1) {
    var zone = shuffledZones[i % shuffledZones.length];
    var street = pickDemoStreetForZone_(zone, ruaRows, rng, zoneStreetCursor);
    var coord = buildDemoCoordinateForZone_(zone, street, rng, 0.00018);
    var logradouro = street && street.logradouro ? street.logradouro : buildSyntheticDemoStreetName_(zone, i + 1);
    var streetKey = zone.zoneKey + '|' + logradouro;
    var nextNumber = (perStreetCounter[streetKey] || 0) + 1;
    perStreetCounter[streetKey] = nextNumber;
    var numero = String((nextNumber * 7) + 9);
    var morador = nameSeed[i % nameSeed.length] + ' ' + surnameSeed[(i * 3) % surnameSeed.length];
    var phoneBase = 970000000 + ((i * 37) % 9999999);

    properties.push({
      uid: 'PROP-DEMO-' + padNumber_(i + 1, 4),
      morador: morador,
      telefone: formatDemoPhone_(phoneBase),
      microarea: zone.microarea,
      quarteirao: zone.quarteirao,
      bairro: zone.bairro,
      logradouro: logradouro,
      numero: numero,
      complemento: i % 11 === 0 ? 'Complemento' : (i % 7 === 0 ? 'Sequencia' : 'Normal'),
      tipo: i % 17 === 0 ? 'Comércio' : 'Residencial',
      referencia: street && street.referencia ? street.referencia : ('Referência demo ' + zone.bairro + (zone.quarteirao ? ' Q' + zone.quarteirao : '')),
      obs: i % 9 === 0 ? 'Imóvel demo para apresentação institucional.' : '',
      address_key: buildDemoAddressKey_(zone.bairro, logradouro, numero),
      last_lat: coord.lat,
      last_lng: coord.lng,
      gps_territory: zone.bairro,
      gps_quarteirao: zone.quarteirao,
      quality_flags: '',
      quality_status: 'demo',
      last_visit_at: '',
      updatedAt: nowIso_()
    });
  }

  return properties;
}

function buildPresentationDemoVisits_(settings, properties, agents, rng) {
  var visits = [];
  var totalVisits = Number(settings.visitCount || 0);
  var depositTarget = Math.round(totalVisits * Number(settings.depositRate || 0));
  var focusTarget = Math.round(totalVisits * Number(settings.focusRate || 0));
  var totalDays = countInclusiveDays_(settings.startDate, settings.endDate);
  var repeatedPropertyUids = [];
  var propertyLastVisitMap = {};
  var propertyByUid = {};
  var agentQueue = shuffleCopy_(agents, rng);
  var closedIndices = pickUniqueIndices_(totalVisits, 70, rng);
  var recoveredIndices = pickUniqueIndicesExcluding_(totalVisits, 35, closedIndices, rng);
  var eligibleForDeposits = invertIndexSet_(totalVisits, mergeIndexSets_([closedIndices]), true);
  var depositIndexList = pickIndicesFromList_(eligibleForDeposits, depositTarget, rng);
  var focusIndexList = pickIndicesFromList_(depositIndexList.slice(), focusTarget, rng);
  var depositIndices = listToIndexSet_(depositIndexList);
  var focusIndices = listToIndexSet_(focusIndexList);

  properties.forEach(function(property) { propertyByUid[property.uid] = property; });

  for (var i = 0; i < totalVisits; i += 1) {
    var property = properties[(i * 37) % properties.length];
    var zoneQuarteirao = String(property.quarteirao || '').trim();
    var date = addDaysToIsoDate_(settings.startDate, Math.floor((i * totalDays) / totalVisits));
    var hour = buildDemoHour_(i, rng);
    var agent = agentQueue[i % agentQueue.length];
    var isClosed = !!closedIndices[i];
    var isRecovered = !isClosed && !!recoveredIndices[i];
    var hasDeposit = !!depositIndices[i];
    var hasFocus = !!focusIndices[i];
    var status = isClosed ? 'Fechado' : (isRecovered ? 'Recuperado' : 'Visitado');
    var closedReason = isClosed ? chooseFromArray_(rng, [
      'Imóvel fechado',
      'Ninguém em casa no momento',
      'Só está em casa à noite',
      'Só está em casa no fim de semana',
      'Morador mora em outra cidade'
    ]) : '';
    var depositPayload = buildDemoDepositPayload_(hasDeposit, hasFocus, rng);
    var waterPayload = buildDemoWaterPayload_(status, hasFocus, depositPayload, rng);
    var gps = buildDemoVisitGps_(property, rng);
    var tubitosQty = hasFocus ? (1 + Math.floor(rng() * 3)) : (hasDeposit && rng() < 0.18 ? 1 : 0);

    visits.push({
      uid: 'VIS-DEMO-' + padNumber_(i + 1, 4),
      data: date,
      hora: hour,
      agente: normalizeText_(agent.nome || agent.matricula || 'ACE Demo'),
      matricula: String(agent.matricula || '').trim(),
      microarea: property.microarea,
      quarteirao: zoneQuarteirao,
      bairro: property.bairro,
      logradouro: property.logradouro,
      numero: property.numero,
      tipo: property.tipo || 'Residencial',
      situacao: status,
      motivo_fechado: closedReason,
      closed_reason: closedReason,
      morador: property.morador,
      telefone: property.telefone,
      property_uid: property.uid,
      deposits: depositPayload.deposits,
      deposit_count: depositPayload.depositCount,
      deposit_focus_breakdown: depositPayload.depositFocusBreakdown,
      deposit_focus_count: depositPayload.depositFocusCount,
      deposit_treatment_breakdown: depositPayload.depositTreatmentBreakdown,
      deposit_treatment_count: depositPayload.depositTreatmentCount,
      deposit_found: hasDeposit ? 'Sim' : 'Não',
      deposit_with_focus: hasFocus ? 'Sim' : 'Não',
      foco: hasFocus ? 'Sim' : 'Não',
      focus_count: hasFocus ? depositPayload.focusTotal : 0,
      acessou_caixa_agua: waterPayload.waterAccess,
      motivo_caixa_agua: waterPayload.waterAccessReason,
      water_access_reason: waterPayload.waterAccessReason,
      solicita_escada: waterPayload.waterAccessReason === 'Necessita escada' ? 'Sim' : '',
      ladder_support_requested: waterPayload.waterAccessReason === 'Necessita escada' ? 'Sim' : '',
      motivo_nao_solicitou_escada: '',
      ladder_support_no_reason: '',
      ladder_support_status: waterPayload.waterAccessReason === 'Necessita escada' ? 'Solicitada' : '',
      situacao_caixa_agua: waterPayload.waterTankCondition,
      water_tank_condition: waterPayload.waterTankCondition,
      tratamento_caixa_agua: waterPayload.waterTreatment,
      water_tank_treatment: waterPayload.waterTreatment,
      attended_by: status === 'Fechado' ? '' : (rng() < 0.82 ? property.morador : chooseFromArray_(rng, ['Morador', 'Familiar', 'Vizinho'])),
      nome_atendido: status === 'Fechado' ? '' : (rng() < 0.82 ? property.morador : chooseFromArray_(rng, ['Familiar responsável', 'Vizinho autorizado'])),
      larvicida: hasFocus ? 'Temefós' : 'Nenhum',
      larvicida_qtd: hasFocus ? (1 + Math.floor(rng() * 2)) : 0,
      adulticida: hasFocus && rng() < 0.2 ? 'Aplicado' : 'Nenhum',
      adulticida_qtd: hasFocus && rng() < 0.2 ? 1 : 0,
      tubitos_qtd: tubitosQty,
      tubitos_deposito: depositPayload.tubitosDeposit,
      obs: buildDemoVisitObservation_(status, hasDeposit, hasFocus, waterPayload),
      gps_lat: gps.lat,
      gps_lng: gps.lng,
      gps_acc: String(4 + Math.floor(rng() * 11)),
      gps_territory: property.gps_territory || property.bairro,
      gps_quarteirao: property.gps_quarteirao || property.quarteirao,
      quality_flags: hasFocus ? 'demo|focus' : 'demo',
      route_url: '',
      updatedAt: date + 'T' + hour + ':00-03:00'
    });

    if (!propertyLastVisitMap[property.uid] || propertyLastVisitMap[property.uid] < date) {
      propertyLastVisitMap[property.uid] = date;
    }
  }

  properties.forEach(function(property) {
    property.last_visit_at = propertyLastVisitMap[property.uid] || '';
    property.updatedAt = (property.last_visit_at || settings.endDate) + 'T18:00:00-03:00';
  });

  return visits;
}

function buildMetricsRowsForDemoVisits_(visits) {
  var grouped = {};
  (visits || []).forEach(function(visit) {
    var date = normalizeDateOnly_(visit.data || '');
    if (!date) { return; }
    if (!grouped[date]) { grouped[date] = []; }
    grouped[date].push(visit);
  });
  return Object.keys(grouped).sort().map(function(date) {
    return buildMetricsFromVisitRows_(date, grouped[date]);
  }).filter(Boolean);
}

function buildMetricsFromVisitRows_(date, visits) {
  if (!date || !visits || !visits.length) { return null; }
  var uniqueProperties = {};
  var openCount = 0;
  var closedCount = 0;
  var recoveredCount = 0;
  var pendingCount = 0;
  var depositsFound = 0;
  var depositsWithFocus = 0;
  var tubitos = 0;
  var gpsCount = 0;
  var focusCount = 0;

  visits.forEach(function(row) {
    var propertyUid = String(row.property_uid || '').trim();
    var addressKey = String(row.logradouro || '').trim() + '|' + String(row.numero || '').trim();
    uniqueProperties[propertyUid || addressKey] = true;
    var situacao = String(row.situacao || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (situacao === 'visitado') { openCount += 1; }
    else if (situacao === 'fechado') { closedCount += 1; }
    else if (situacao === 'recuperado') { recoveredCount += 1; }
    else { pendingCount += 1; }
    depositsFound += toNumber_(row.deposit_count || 0);
    depositsWithFocus += toNumber_(row.deposit_focus_count || 0);
    tubitos += toNumber_(row.tubitos_qtd || 0);
    focusCount += toNumber_(row.focus_count || 0);
    if (String(row.gps_lat || '').trim() && String(row.gps_lng || '').trim()) { gpsCount += 1; }
  });

  var total = openCount + closedCount + recoveredCount + pendingCount;
  var visitedProperties = Object.keys(uniqueProperties).length;
  return sanitizeObjectForHeaders_({
    reference_date: date,
    total_visitas: visits.length,
    imoveis_visitados: visitedProperties,
    abertos: openCount,
    fechados: closedCount,
    total: total,
    recuperados: recoveredCount,
    pendencias: pendingCount,
    depositos_encontrados: depositsFound,
    depositos_com_foco: depositsWithFocus,
    tubitos_qtd: tubitos,
    taxa_infestacao: visitedProperties ? Number((focusCount / visitedProperties).toFixed(4)) : 0,
    cobertura_gps: total ? Number((gpsCount / total).toFixed(4)) : 0,
    retornos: recoveredCount,
    focos: focusCount
  }, ACE_API.HEADERS.MetricsDaily);
}

function buildDemoDepositPayload_(hasDeposit, hasFocus, rng) {
  var codes = ['A1', 'A2', 'B', 'C', 'D1', 'D2', 'E'];
  var depositMap = {};
  var focusMap = {};
  var treatmentMap = {};
  var depositCount = 0;
  var focusTotal = 0;
  var tubitosDeposit = '';

  codes.forEach(function(code) {
    depositMap[code] = 0;
    focusMap[code] = 0;
    treatmentMap[code] = 0;
  });

  if (!hasDeposit) {
    return {
      deposits: '',
      depositCount: 0,
      depositFocusBreakdown: '',
      depositFocusCount: 0,
      depositTreatmentBreakdown: '',
      depositTreatmentCount: 0,
      focusTotal: 0,
      tubitosDeposit: ''
    };
  }

  var selectedCodes = shuffleCopy_(codes, rng).slice(0, 1 + Math.floor(rng() * 3));
  if (hasFocus && selectedCodes.indexOf('A1') === -1 && rng() < 0.55) {
    selectedCodes[0] = 'A1';
  }

  selectedCodes.forEach(function(code, index) {
    var qty = 1 + Math.floor(rng() * (code === 'A1' ? 3 : 2));
    depositMap[code] = qty;
    depositCount += qty;

    if (hasFocus && (index === 0 || rng() < 0.45)) {
      var focusQty = 1 + Math.floor(rng() * Math.max(1, qty));
      focusMap[code] = focusQty;
      focusTotal += focusQty;
      if (!tubitosDeposit) { tubitosDeposit = code; }
    }

    if (rng() < 0.65) {
      treatmentMap[code] = Math.max(focusMap[code], 1 + Math.floor(rng() * qty));
    }
  });

  if (hasFocus && !focusTotal) {
    focusMap[selectedCodes[0]] = 1;
    focusTotal = 1;
    tubitosDeposit = selectedCodes[0];
  }

  return {
    deposits: compactDemoMap_(depositMap),
    depositCount: depositCount,
    depositFocusBreakdown: compactDemoMap_(focusMap),
    depositFocusCount: sumDemoMap_(focusMap),
    depositTreatmentBreakdown: compactDemoMap_(treatmentMap),
    depositTreatmentCount: sumDemoMap_(treatmentMap),
    focusTotal: focusTotal,
    tubitosDeposit: tubitosDeposit
  };
}

function buildDemoWaterPayload_(status, hasFocus, depositPayload, rng) {
  if (status === 'Fechado') {
    var reasonClosed = chooseFromArray_(rng, [
      'Sem acesso ao imóvel',
      'Morador não autorizou',
      'Necessita escada',
      'Local de difícil acesso'
    ]);
    return {
      waterAccess: 'Não',
      waterAccessReason: reasonClosed,
      waterTankCondition: '',
      waterTreatment: ''
    };
  }

  var access = hasFocus ? 'Sim' : (rng() < 0.76 ? 'Sim' : 'Não');
  if (access === 'Não') {
    return {
      waterAccess: 'Não',
      waterAccessReason: chooseFromArray_(rng, [
        'Sem acesso ao imóvel',
        'Caixa dentro do forro/teto',
        'Necessita escada',
        'Morador não autorizou',
        'Não existe caixa d\'água no local'
      ]),
      waterTankCondition: '',
      waterTreatment: ''
    };
  }

  var condition = hasFocus
    ? (rng() < 0.65 ? 'Aberta / vulnerável' : 'Com água de chuva')
    : chooseFromArray_(rng, ['Tampada', 'Com tela', 'Aberta / vulnerável', 'Vazia', 'Com água de chuva']);

  var treatment = hasFocus || depositPayload.depositTreatmentCount > 0
    ? (rng() < 0.58 ? 'Sim' : 'Não')
    : (rng() < 0.15 ? 'Sim' : 'Não');

  return {
    waterAccess: 'Sim',
    waterAccessReason: '',
    waterTankCondition: condition,
    waterTreatment: treatment
  };
}

function buildDemoVisitObservation_(status, hasDeposit, hasFocus, waterPayload) {
  var parts = ['Visita fictícia gerada para apresentação.'];
  if (status === 'Fechado') {
    parts.push('Retorno sugerido para nova tentativa.');
  }
  if (hasDeposit) {
    parts.push('Depósitos lançados na simulação.');
  }
  if (hasFocus) {
    parts.push('Foco positivo simulado.');
  }
  if (waterPayload && waterPayload.waterAccess === 'Sim' && waterPayload.waterTankCondition) {
    parts.push('Caixa d\'água: ' + waterPayload.waterTankCondition + '.');
  }
  return parts.join(' ');
}

function pickDemoStreetForZone_(zone, ruaRows, rng, zoneStreetCursor) {
  var candidates = (ruaRows || []).filter(function(row) {
    return scoreDemoStreetForZone_(row, zone) > 0;
  }).sort(function(a, b) {
    return scoreDemoStreetForZone_(b, zone) - scoreDemoStreetForZone_(a, zone);
  });

  if (!candidates.length) {
    return null;
  }

  var cursor = zoneStreetCursor[zone.zoneKey] || 0;
  zoneStreetCursor[zone.zoneKey] = cursor + 1;
  return candidates[cursor % candidates.length];
}

function scoreDemoStreetForZone_(row, zone) {
  if (!row || !String(row.logradouro || '').trim()) { return 0; }
  var score = 1;
  var zoneTerritoryKey = normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(zone.territoryName || zone.bairro || zone.microarea || '');
  var bairroBaseKey = normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(row.bairro_base || '');
  if (bairroBaseKey && bairroBaseKey === zoneTerritoryKey) {
    score += 5;
  }

  var microareaMatches = extractDemoPipeValues_(row.microareas_sugeridas || '').some(function(item) {
    return normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(item) === zoneTerritoryKey || normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(item) === normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(zone.bairro) || normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(item) === normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(zone.microarea);
  });
  if (microareaMatches) {
    score += 5;
  }

  var quarterCodes = extractQuarterCodesForDemo_(row.quarteiroes_sugeridos || row.quarteirao_base || '');
  if (zone.quarteirao && quarterCodes.indexOf(String(zone.quarteirao || '')) > -1) {
    score += 6;
  } else if (!zone.quarteirao && !quarterCodes.length) {
    score += 2;
  }

  if (String(row.latitude_ref || '').trim() && String(row.longitude_ref || '').trim()) {
    score += 2;
  }

  return score;
}

function buildSyntheticDemoStreetName_(zone, index) {
  return 'Rua Demo ' + (zone.bairro || zone.territoryName || 'Território') + (zone.quarteirao ? ' Q' + zone.quarteirao : '') + ' ' + padNumber_(index, 3);
}

function buildDemoCoordinateForZone_(zone, street, rng, amplitude) {
  var baseLat = toNumber_(street && street.latitude_ref ? street.latitude_ref : zone.lat);
  var baseLng = toNumber_(street && street.longitude_ref ? street.longitude_ref : zone.lng);
  if (!baseLat && zone.lat) { baseLat = zone.lat; }
  if (!baseLng && zone.lng) { baseLng = zone.lng; }
  var lat = baseLat + ((rng() - 0.5) * amplitude * 2);
  var lng = baseLng + ((rng() - 0.5) * amplitude * 2);
  return {
    lat: Number(lat.toFixed(6)),
    lng: Number(lng.toFixed(6))
  };
}

function buildDemoVisitGps_(property, rng) {
  var lat = toNumber_(property.last_lat || 0);
  var lng = toNumber_(property.last_lng || 0);
  return {
    lat: Number((lat + ((rng() - 0.5) * 0.00008 * 2)).toFixed(6)),
    lng: Number((lng + ((rng() - 0.5) * 0.00008 * 2)).toFixed(6))
  };
}

function normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(value) {
  var key = normalizeComparableText_(stripMicroareaPrefix_(value || ''));
  return ACE_DEMO_TERRITORY_ALIASES[key] || key;
}

function getDemoBairroLabelFromTerritory_(value) {
  var key = normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(value || '');
  return ACE_DEMO_TERRITORY_BAIRRO_LABELS[key] || normalizeBairroValue_(String(value || '').trim());
}

function getDemoMicroareaLabelFromTerritory_(value) {
  var key = normalizeDemoTerritoryKey_(value || '');
  return ACE_DEMO_TERRITORY_MICROAREA_LABELS[key] || normalizeMicroareaValue_(String(value || '').trim(), String(value || '').trim());
}

function buildDemoAddressKey_(bairro, logradouro, numero) {
  return [normalizeComparableText_(bairro), normalizeComparableText_(logradouro), normalizeComparableText_(numero)].join('|');
}

function extractDemoPipeValues_(value) {
  return String(value || '').split('|').map(function(item) {
    return String(item || '').trim();
  }).filter(Boolean);
}

function extractQuarterCodesForDemo_(value) {
  var list = [];
  String(value || '').replace(/(\d+(?:\/\d+)?)/g, function(_, match) {
    var code = String(match || '').trim();
    if (code && list.indexOf(code) === -1) {
      list.push(code);
    }
    return match;
  });
  return list;
}

function compactDemoMap_(map) {
  return Object.keys(map || {}).filter(function(code) {
    return Number((map || {})[code] || 0) > 0;
  }).map(function(code) {
    return code + '(' + Number(map[code] || 0) + ')';
  }).join('|');
}

function sumDemoMap_(map) {
  return Object.keys(map || {}).reduce(function(total, key) {
    return total + Number((map || {})[key] || 0);
  }, 0);
}

function buildDemoHour_(index, rng) {
  var slots = ['07:35', '08:10', '08:55', '09:40', '10:25', '11:05', '13:20', '14:05', '14:50', '15:35', '16:15'];
  return slots[index % slots.length];
}

function formatDemoPhone_(numberValue) {
  var digits = String(Math.abs(Math.floor(numberValue || 0))).replace(/\D+/g, '');
  digits = digits.slice(-9).padStart(9, '0');
  return '(22) 9' + digits.slice(-8, -4) + '-' + digits.slice(-4);
}

function padNumber_(value, length) {
  var text = String(value == null ? '' : value);
  while (text.length < length) {
    text = '0' + text;
  }
  return text;
}

function createSeededRandom_(seed) {
  var state = Math.floor(Math.abs(Number(seed || 1))) % 2147483647;
  if (state <= 0) { state += 2147483646; }
  return function() {
    state = (state * 16807) % 2147483647;
    return (state - 1) / 2147483646;
  };
}

function shuffleCopy_(values, rng) {
  var copy = (values || []).slice();
  for (var i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i -= 1) {
    var j = Math.floor(rng() * (i + 1));
    var temp = copy[i];
    copy[i] = copy[j];
    copy[j] = temp;
  }
  return copy;
}

function chooseFromArray_(rng, values) {
  var list = values || [];
  if (!list.length) { return ''; }
  return list[Math.floor(rng() * list.length)];
}

function countInclusiveDays_(startDate, endDate) {
  var start = new Date(startDate + 'T00:00:00-03:00');
  var end = new Date(endDate + 'T00:00:00-03:00');
  return Math.max(1, Math.round((end.getTime() - start.getTime()) / 86400000) + 1);
}

function addDaysToIsoDate_(startDate, daysToAdd) {
  var date = new Date(startDate + 'T00:00:00-03:00');
  date.setDate(date.getDate() + Number(daysToAdd || 0));
  return Utilities.formatDate(date, Session.getScriptTimeZone() || 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
}

function pickUniqueIndices_(total, target, rng) {
  var indices = {};
  var max = Math.min(Number(target || 0), Number(total || 0));
  while (Object.keys(indices).length < max) {
    indices[Math.floor(rng() * total)] = true;
  }
  return indices;
}

function pickUniqueIndicesExcluding_(total, target, excludedMap, rng) {
  var allowed = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < total; i += 1) {
    if (!excludedMap[i]) {
      allowed.push(i);
    }
  }
  return listToIndexSet_(pickIndicesFromList_(allowed, target, rng));
}

function invertIndexSet_(total, excludedMap, returnList) {
  var out = returnList ? [] : {};
  for (var i = 0; i < total; i += 1) {
    if (!excludedMap[i]) {
      if (returnList) { out.push(i); } else { out[i] = true; }
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function pickIndicesFromList_(list, target, rng) {
  var shuffled = shuffleCopy_(list || [], rng);
  return shuffled.slice(0, Math.min(Number(target || 0), shuffled.length));
}

function mergeIndexSets_(maps) {
  var out = {};
  (maps || []).forEach(function(map) {
    Object.keys(map || {}).forEach(function(key) { out[key] = true; });
  });
  return out;
}

function listToIndexSet_(list) {
  var out = {};
  (list || []).forEach(function(index) { out[index] = true; });
  return out;
}


function migrateCanonicalCaixaDAguaData_() {
  setupProductionSheets();

  var counts = {
    bairros: 0,
    ruas: 0,
    agentes: 0,
    imoveis: 0,
    visitas: 0
  };

  var bairros = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.BAIRROS).map(function(row) {
    return sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(row, ACE_API.HEADERS.BairrosCatalogo);
  });

  if (bairros.length) {
    var seenBairros = {};
    var dedupedBairros = [];
    bairros.forEach(function(row) {
      var key = normalizeComparableText_(row.nome || '');
      if (!key || seenBairros[key]) { return; }
      seenBairros[key] = true;
      row.nome = normalizeBairroValue_(row.nome);
      dedupedBairros.push(row);
    });
    writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.BAIRROS, dedupedBairros, ACE_API.HEADERS.BairrosCatalogo);
    counts.bairros = dedupedBairros.length;
  }

  var ruas = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.RUAS).map(function(row) {
    return sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(row, ACE_API.HEADERS.RuasBase);
  });
  if (ruas.length) {
    writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.RUAS, ruas, ACE_API.HEADERS.RuasBase);
    counts.ruas = ruas.length;
  }

  var agentes = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES).map(function(row) {
    return sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(row, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  });
  if (agentes.length) {
    writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, agentes, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
    counts.agentes = agentes.length;
  }

  var imoveis = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS).map(function(row) {
    return sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(row, ACE_API.HEADERS.Imoveis);
  });
  if (imoveis.length) {
    writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS, imoveis, ACE_API.HEADERS.Imoveis);
    counts.imoveis = imoveis.length;
  }

  var visitas = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS).map(function(row) {
    return sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(row, ACE_API.HEADERS.Visitas);
  });
  if (visitas.length) {
    writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS, visitas, ACE_API.HEADERS.Visitas);
    counts.visitas = visitas.length;
  }

  appendLogSafe_(
    'migration',
    'padronizacao_caixa_dagua',
    'SYSTEM',
    'Padronizacao executada para bairro/microarea Caixa d\'Água. ' + JSON.stringify(counts)
  );

  getTargetSpreadsheet_().toast("Padronização de Caixa d'Água concluída.", 'ACE Campo', 6);

  return {
    ok: true,
    canonical_bairro: ACE_CANONICAL_TERRITORY.BAIRRO_LABEL,
    canonical_microarea: ACE_CANONICAL_TERRITORY.MICROAREA_LABEL,
    counts: counts
  };
}

function doGet(e) {
  e = e || {};
  var acePanelResponse = ACE_tryHandlePanelGet_(e);
  if (acePanelResponse) {
    return acePanelResponse;
  }
  var p = e.parameter || {};
  var action = normalizeApiAction_(p.action || 'status');

  try {
    ensureProductionSheetsCached_();

    if (action === 'admin_auth' || action === 'panel_admin_auth') {
      if (!ACE_allowLegacyPanelHashAccess_()) {
        return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_({
          ok: false,
          authenticated: false,
          admin: false,
          error: 'Autenticação administrativa pública desativada. Use o login privado do painel.',
          generatedAt: nowIso_()
        }));
      }
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_(authenticatePanelAdmin_(p)));
    }

    if (action === 'status') {
      return respond_(p.callback, buildPublicStatusPayload_());
    }

    if (action === 'status_full' || action === 'status_admin' || action === 'admin_status') {
      requireApiTokenOrAdminSession_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, buildStatusPayload_());
    }

    if (action === 'bootstrap') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      setSystemState_('last_bootstrap_at', nowIso_());
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_({
        ok: true,
        generatedAt: nowIso_(),
        agents: buildAgentsForResponse_(buildVisibleAgentRoster_(), p),
        properties: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS),
        tubitos: buildTubitosForResponse_(readLaboratoryTubitoRows_()),
        operation_plan: getActiveOperationPlanPayload_(p),
        operation_schedule: getDailyOperationSchedulePayload_(p),
        operation_workday_settings: getOperationWorkdaySettings_(),
        catalogs: { bairros: readBairrosCatalog_() },
        meta: { counts: getCounts_() }
      }));
    }

    if (action === 'roster' || action === 'login_bootstrap') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_({
        ok: true,
        generatedAt: nowIso_(),
        agents: buildAgentsForResponse_(buildVisibleAgentRoster_(), p),
        catalogs: { bairros: readBairrosCatalog_() }
      }));
    }

    if (action === 'properties_catalog' || action === 'properties') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_({
        ok: true,
        generatedAt: nowIso_(),
        properties: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS),
        catalogs: { bairros: readBairrosCatalog_() }
      }));
    }

    if (action === 'catalogs') {
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_({
        ok: true,
        generatedAt: nowIso_(),
        bairros: readBairrosCatalog_(),
        catalogs: { bairros: readBairrosCatalog_() }
      }));
    }

    if (action === 'liraa_plan' || action === 'liraa_active_plan') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_(getActiveLiraaPlanPayload_()));
    }

    if (action === 'liraa_cycle_history' || action === 'liraa_cycles' || action === 'historico_liraa') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_(getLiraaCycleHistoryPayload_(p)));
    }

    if (action === 'operation_plan' || action === 'active_operation_plan' || action === 'modo_operacao') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_(getActiveOperationPlanPayload_(p)));
    }

    if (action === 'operation_schedule' || action === 'operations_day' || action === 'operacoes_dia') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_(getDailyOperationSchedulePayload_(p)));
    }

    if (action === 'operation_workday_settings' || action === 'operation_jornada' || action === 'jornada_operacao') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_({
        ok: true,
        generatedAt: nowIso_(),
        workday_settings: getOperationWorkdaySettings_()
      }));
    }

    if (action === 'tubitos' || action === 'laboratorio_tubitos' || action === 'lab_tubitos') {
      assertLaboratoryAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_({
        ok: true,
        generatedAt: nowIso_(),
        tubitos: buildTubitosForResponse_(readLaboratoryTubitoRows_())
      }));
    }

    if (action === 'dashboard_range') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_(buildDashboardResponse_(p, { summaryOnly: false })));
    }

    if (action === 'dashboard_summary') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_(buildDashboardResponse_(p, { summaryOnly: true })));
    }

    if (action === 'attendance_summary' || action === 'agent_attendance' || action === 'frequencia_agentes') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_(buildAttendanceSummary_(p)));
    }

    if (action === 'supervision_requests' || action === 'solicitacoes_supervisao') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_({
        ok: true,
        generatedAt: nowIso_(),
        supervision_requests: filterSupervisionRequestRows_(p)
      }));
    }

    if (action === 'backup_export') {
      requireApiTokenOrAdminSession_(p);
      return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_(buildBackupExportPayload_()));
    }

    return respond_(p.callback, buildPublicStatusPayload_());
  } catch (err) {
    return respond_(p.callback, wrapPayload_({ ok: false, error: safeErrorMessage_(err), generatedAt: nowIso_() }));
  }
}


function sanitizeSupervisionRequestPayload_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var now = nowIso_();
  var date = normalizeDateOnly_(payload.data || payload.date || now) || normalizeDateOnly_(now);
  var time = String(payload.hora || payload.time || '').trim();
  if (!time) {
    time = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone() || 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'HH:mm');
  }
  return sanitizeObjectForHeaders_({
    uid: String(payload.uid || ('SUP-' + Utilities.getUuid())).trim(),
    data: date,
    hora: time.slice(0, 5),
    agente: String(payload.agente || payload.agent || payload.nome_agente || '').trim(),
    matricula: String(payload.matricula || payload.agent_code || '').trim(),
    operation_mode: String(payload.operation_mode || payload.operational_mode || payload.origem_visita || 'VD').trim() || 'VD',
    mensagem: String(payload.mensagem || payload.message || 'Agente solicitou supervisão em campo.').trim(),
    status: String(payload.status || 'Aberta').trim() || 'Aberta',
    gps_lat: payload.gps_lat || payload.gpsLat || payload.latitude || payload.lat || '',
    gps_lng: payload.gps_lng || payload.gpsLng || payload.longitude || payload.lng || '',
    gps_acc: payload.gps_acc || payload.gpsAccuracy || payload.accuracy || '',
    gps_territory: payload.gps_territory || payload.gpsTerritory || '',
    gps_quarteirao: payload.gps_quarteirao || payload.gpsQuarteirao || '',
    createdAt: payload.createdAt || payload.created_at || now,
    updatedAt: payload.updatedAt || payload.updated_at || now
  }, ACE_API.HEADERS.SupervisionRequests);
}

function saveSupervisionRequest_(payload) {
  var row = sanitizeSupervisionRequestPayload_(payload);
  if (!row.agente && !row.matricula) {
    throw new Error('Agente não informado na solicitação de supervisão.');
  }
  if (!row.gps_lat || !row.gps_lng) {
    throw new Error('Localização não informada na solicitação de supervisão.');
  }
  var count = upsertRows_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SUPERVISION, [row], 'uid', ACE_API.HEADERS.SupervisionRequests);
  appendLogSafe_('supervisao', 'request', row.uid, 'Solicitação de supervisão enviada por ' + (row.agente || row.matricula || 'agente') + '.');
  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    count: count,
    request: row
  };
}

function normalizeOperationalRequestStatus_(status) {
  var value = String(status || '').trim();
  var comparable = normalizeComparableText_(value);
  if (comparable.indexOf('cancel') > -1) {
    return 'Cancelada';
  }
  if (comparable.indexOf('conclu') > -1 || comparable.indexOf('resolvid') > -1 || comparable === 'fechada' || comparable === 'fechado') {
    return 'Concluída';
  }
  if (comparable.indexOf('atendimento') > -1 || comparable.indexOf('andamento') > -1) {
    return 'Em atendimento';
  }
  return 'Aberta';
}

function updateSupervisionRequestStatus_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  ACE_panelRequirePrivateSession_(Object.assign({}, payload, { access_module: 'supervisao', accessModule: 'supervisao' }));
  var uid = String(payload.uid || payload.request_uid || payload.requestId || '').trim();
  if (!uid) {
    throw new Error('UID da solicitação de supervisão não informado.');
  }
  var status = normalizeOperationalRequestStatus_(payload.status || payload.new_status || 'Aberta');
  var rows = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SUPERVISION);
  var found = null;
  rows.forEach(function(row) {
    if (String(row.uid || '').trim() === uid) {
      row.status = status;
      row.updatedAt = payload.updatedAt || payload.updated_at || nowIso_();
      found = row;
    }
  });
  if (!found) {
    throw new Error('Solicitação de supervisão não encontrada.');
  }
  upsertRows_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SUPERVISION, [sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(found, ACE_API.HEADERS.SupervisionRequests)], 'uid', ACE_API.HEADERS.SupervisionRequests);
  appendLogSafe_('supervisao', 'status', uid, 'Status da solicitação de supervisão atualizado para ' + status + '.');
  return { ok: true, generatedAt: nowIso_(), uid: uid, status: status };
}

function updateLadderRequestStatus_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  ACE_panelRequirePrivateSession_(Object.assign({}, payload, { access_module: 'supervisao', accessModule: 'supervisao' }));
  var uid = String(payload.uid || payload.visit_uid || payload.visitId || '').trim();
  if (!uid) {
    throw new Error('UID da visita/pedido de escada não informado.');
  }
  var status = normalizeOperationalRequestStatus_(payload.status || payload.new_status || 'Aberta');
  var rows = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS);
  var found = null;
  rows.forEach(function(row) {
    if (String(row.uid || '').trim() === uid) {
      row.ladder_support_status = status;
      row.updatedAt = payload.updatedAt || payload.updated_at || nowIso_();
      found = row;
    }
  });
  if (!found) {
    throw new Error('Pedido de escada não encontrado.');
  }
  upsertVisitRowsPreservingLab_([sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(found, ACE_API.HEADERS.Visitas)]);
  appendLogSafe_('escada', 'status', uid, 'Status do pedido de escada atualizado para ' + status + '.');
  return { ok: true, generatedAt: nowIso_(), uid: uid, status: status };
}


function ACE_supervisionResolvedEarlyClosureMap_() {
  var raw = getSystemState_('supervision_resolved_early_closures');
  if (!raw) { return {}; }
  try {
    var parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? parsed : {};
  } catch (error) {
    return {};
  }
}

function ACE_panelEarlyClosureUidFromLog_(row) {
  row = row || {};
  var timestamp = ACE_panelText_(row.timestamp || row.createdAt || row.created_at);
  return ACE_panelText_(row.uid || row.ref_uid || row.refUid || ('EARLY-' + timestamp));
}

function updateEarlyClosureStatus_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var session = ACE_panelRequirePrivateSession_(Object.assign({}, payload, { access_module: 'supervisao', accessModule: 'supervisao' }));
  var uid = String(payload.uid || payload.early_uid || payload.ref_uid || '').trim();
  if (!uid) {
    throw new Error('UID do encerramento antecipado não informado.');
  }

  var status = normalizeOperationalRequestStatus_(payload.status || payload.new_status || 'Concluída');
  var resolved = ACE_supervisionResolvedEarlyClosureMap_();
  if (status === 'Concluída') {
    resolved[uid] = {
      status: status,
      updatedAt: payload.updatedAt || payload.updated_at || nowIso_(),
      actor: normalizeText_(session.nome || session.matricula || 'supervisao')
    };
  } else {
    delete resolved[uid];
  }

  setSystemState_('supervision_resolved_early_closures', resolved);
  appendLogSafe_('supervisao', 'early_close_status', uid, 'Aviso de encerramento antecipado atualizado para ' + status + '.');
  return { ok: true, generatedAt: nowIso_(), uid: uid, status: status };
}

function filterSupervisionRequestRows_(params) {
  params = params || {};
  var fromDate = normalizeDateOnly_(params.from || params.start || '');
  var toDate = normalizeDateOnly_(params.to || params.end || '');
  return readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SUPERVISION).filter(function(row) {
    var date = normalizeDateOnly_(row.data || row.date || '');
    if (fromDate && date && date < fromDate) { return false; }
    if (toDate && date && date > toDate) { return false; }
    return true;
  }).sort(function(a, b) {
    return String((b.data || '') + ' ' + (b.hora || '') + ' ' + (b.updatedAt || '')).localeCompare(String((a.data || '') + ' ' + (a.hora || '') + ' ' + (a.updatedAt || '')));
  });
}


function ACE_adminNormalizeManagedRole_(value) {
  var normalized = normalizeRole_(value || '');
  if (normalized === 'administrador' || normalized === 'admin') { return 'Administrador'; }
  if (normalized === 'supervisor' || normalized === 'supervisao' || normalized === 'supervisão') { return 'Supervisor'; }
  if (normalized === 'coordenador' || normalized === 'coord' || normalized === 'coordenacao' || normalized === 'coordenação') { return 'Coordenador'; }
  if (normalized === 'secretaria') { return 'Secretaria'; }
  if (normalized === 'atendente' || normalized === 'administrativo') { return 'Atendente'; }
  return 'ACE';
}

function ACE_adminRequireAdministrator_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var request = Object.assign({}, payload, { access_module: 'administrador' });
  var session = ACE_panelRequirePrivateSession_(request);
  if (normalizeRole_(session.role || '') !== 'administrador') {
    throw new Error('Apenas Administrador pode criar, editar ou remover usuários.');
  }
  return session;
}

function ACE_adminPublicAgentRow_(row) {
  row = row || {};
  return {
    uid: String(row.uid || '').trim(),
    nome: normalizeText_(row.nome || ''),
    matricula: String(row.matricula || '').trim(),
    cpf: normalizeCpfValue_(row.cpf || ''),
    role: ACE_adminNormalizeManagedRole_(row.role || 'ACE'),
    base_microarea: normalizeMicroareaValue_(row.base_microarea || row.baseMicroarea || '', row.base_region || row.baseRegion || ''),
    base_region: normalizeText_(row.base_region || row.baseRegion || ''),
    updatedAt: String(row.updatedAt || '').trim()
  };
}

function ACE_adminFindAgentIndex_(rows, identity) {
  identity = identity || {};
  var uid = String(identity.uid || '').trim();
  var matricula = String(identity.matricula || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  var cpf = normalizeCpfValue_(identity.cpf || '');
  var index = -1;
  (rows || []).some(function(row, i) {
    var rowUid = String(row.uid || '').trim();
    var rowMatricula = String(row.matricula || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    var rowCpf = normalizeCpfValue_(row.cpf || '');
    if ((uid && rowUid === uid) || (matricula && rowMatricula === matricula) || (cpf && rowCpf === cpf)) {
      index = i;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  });
  return index;
}

function ACE_adminBuildUid_(row) {
  var cpf = normalizeCpfValue_(row.cpf || '');
  var matricula = String(row.matricula || '').trim().toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9_-]+/g, '');
  if (cpf) { return 'AGT-' + cpf; }
  if (matricula) { return 'AGT-' + matricula; }
  return 'AGT-' + Utilities.getUuid();
}

function ACE_adminValidateAgentInput_(row, existing) {
  row = row || {};
  existing = existing || {};
  var nome = normalizeText_(row.nome || row.name || '');
  var matricula = String(row.matricula || row.codigo || row.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  var cpf = normalizeCpfValue_(row.cpf || row.documento || '');
  var role = ACE_adminNormalizeManagedRole_(row.role || row.perfil || 'ACE');
  var baseMicroarea = normalizeMicroareaValue_(row.base_microarea || row.baseMicroarea || '', row.base_region || row.baseRegion || '');
  var baseRegion = normalizeText_(row.base_region || row.baseRegion || '');
  var password = String(row.senha || row.password || row.temporaryPassword || '').trim();
  var passwordHash = String(row.senhaHash || row.password_hash || '').trim();

  if (!nome) { throw new Error('Informe o nome do usuário.'); }
  if (!matricula) { throw new Error('Informe a matrícula/código do usuário.'); }
  if (!cpf || cpf.length !== 11) { throw new Error('Informe um CPF válido com 11 dígitos.'); }

  return {
    uid: String(row.uid || existing.uid || '').trim() || ACE_adminBuildUid_({ cpf: cpf, matricula: matricula }),
    nome: nome,
    matricula: matricula,
    cpf: cpf,
    role: role,
    base_microarea: baseMicroarea,
    base_region: baseRegion,
    senhaHash: password ? sha256Hex_(password) : (passwordHash || String(existing.senhaHash || '').trim()),
    updatedAt: nowIso_()
  };
}

function ACE_adminListAgents_(payload) {
  ACE_adminRequireAdministrator_(payload || {});
  var rows = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES)
    .filter(function(row) { return !isRemovedAgentRow_(row); })
    .map(ACE_adminPublicAgentRow_);
  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    agents: rows
  };
}

function ACE_adminSaveAgent_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var session = ACE_adminRequireAdministrator_(payload);
  var incoming = payload.agent || payload.usuario || payload.user || {};
  var rows = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES).filter(function(row) {
    return !isRemovedAgentRow_(row);
  });
  var index = ACE_adminFindAgentIndex_(rows, incoming);
  var existing = index > -1 ? rows[index] : {};
  var normalized = ACE_adminValidateAgentInput_(incoming, existing);

  if (!normalized.senhaHash) {
    throw new Error('Informe uma senha temporária para novo usuário ou redefina a senha.');
  }

  var duplicateCpf = rows.some(function(row, i) {
    return i !== index && normalizeCpfValue_(row.cpf || '') === normalized.cpf;
  });
  if (duplicateCpf) { throw new Error('Já existe usuário com este CPF.'); }

  var duplicateMatricula = rows.some(function(row, i) {
    return i !== index && String(row.matricula || '').trim().toUpperCase() === normalized.matricula;
  });
  if (duplicateMatricula) { throw new Error('Já existe usuário com esta matrícula/código.'); }

  if (index > -1) {
    rows[index] = Object.assign({}, existing, normalized);
  } else {
    rows.push(normalized);
  }

  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, rows, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  appendLogSafe_('admin', index > -1 ? 'agent_update' : 'agent_create', normalized.uid, 'Usuário ' + normalized.nome + ' salvo por ' + normalizeText_(session.nome || session.matricula || 'Administrador') + '.');

  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    saved: ACE_adminPublicAgentRow_(normalized),
    agents: rows.map(ACE_adminPublicAgentRow_)
  };
}

function ACE_adminDeleteAgent_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var session = ACE_adminRequireAdministrator_(payload);
  var identity = payload.agent || payload.usuario || payload.user || payload;
  var rows = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES).filter(function(row) {
    return !isRemovedAgentRow_(row);
  });
  var index = ACE_adminFindAgentIndex_(rows, identity);
  if (index < 0) {
    throw new Error('Usuário não encontrado para remoção.');
  }

  var target = rows[index];
  if (normalizeCpfValue_(target.cpf || '') && normalizeCpfValue_(target.cpf || '') === normalizeCpfValue_(session.cpf || '')) {
    throw new Error('Você não pode remover o próprio usuário administrador logado.');
  }

  var remainingAdmins = rows.filter(function(row, i) {
    return i !== index && normalizeRole_(row.role || '') === 'administrador' && String(row.senhaHash || '').trim();
  }).length;
  if (normalizeRole_(target.role || '') === 'administrador' && remainingAdmins < 1) {
    throw new Error('Não é permitido remover o último administrador ativo.');
  }

  rows.splice(index, 1);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, rows, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  appendLogSafe_('admin', 'agent_delete', String(target.uid || target.matricula || '').trim(), 'Usuário ' + normalizeText_(target.nome || target.matricula || '') + ' removido por ' + normalizeText_(session.nome || session.matricula || 'Administrador') + '.');

  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    deleted: ACE_adminPublicAgentRow_(target),
    agents: rows.map(ACE_adminPublicAgentRow_)
  };
}


function doPost(e) {
  var lock = null;
  try {
    setupProductionSheets();
    var payload = parseJsonBody_(e);
    var action = normalizeApiAction_(payload.action || '');

    if (action === 'login' || action === 'authenticate') {
      return jsonResponse_(authenticateAgent_(payload));
    }

    if (action === 'field_session_status' || action === 'operational_session_status') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_fieldPrivateSessionStatus_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'field_session_refresh' || action === 'operational_session_refresh') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_fieldRefreshPrivateSession_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'field_logout' || action === 'operational_logout') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_fieldPrivateLogout_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'panel_login' || action === 'panel_private_login') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_panelPrivateLogin_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'panel_bundle_private' || action === 'panel_private_bundle') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_panelPrivateBundle_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'secretaria_bundle_private' || action === 'secretaria_private_bundle') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_secretariaPrivateBundle_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'secretaria_document_save' || action === 'secretaria_emit_document' || action === 'secretaria_document_emit') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_secretariaSaveDocument_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'panel_logout' || action === 'panel_private_logout') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_panelPrivateLogout_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'panel_session_status' || action === 'panel_private_session_status') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_panelPrivateSessionStatus_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'panel_session_refresh' || action === 'panel_private_session_refresh') {
