      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_panelRefreshPrivateSession_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'admin_agents_list' || action === 'admin_users_list') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_adminListAgents_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'admin_agent_save' || action === 'admin_user_save') {
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_adminSaveAgent_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'admin_agent_delete' || action === 'admin_user_delete') {
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(ACE_adminDeleteAgent_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'reset_operational') {
      requireApiTokenOrAdminSession_(payload);
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(resetOperationalData_()));
    }

    if (action === 'reset_access') {
      requireApiTokenOrAdminSession_(payload);
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(resetAgentsAndRecreateAdmin_()));
    }

    if (action === 'reset_clean_base') {
      requireApiTokenOrAdminSession_(payload);
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(resetCleanBasePreservingReferenceData_()));
    }

    if (action === 'diagnose_active_spreadsheet') {
      requireApiTokenOrAdminSession_(payload);
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(diagnosticarBancoAtivo_()));
    }

    if (action === 'generate_demo_presentation') {
      requireApiTokenOrAdminSession_(payload);
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(generatePresentationDemoData_()));
    }

    if (action === 'restore_backup') {
      requireApiTokenOrAdminSession_(payload);
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(restoreBackupPayload_(payload.snapshot || payload.backup || {})));
    }

    if (action === 'liraa_plan_publish' || action === 'publish_liraa_plan') {
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(publishLiraaPlan_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'liraa_plan_clear' || action === 'clear_liraa_plan') {
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(clearActiveLiraaPlan_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'liraa_cycle_archive' || action === 'archive_liraa_cycle' || action === 'liraa_cycle_save') {
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(archiveLiraaCycle_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'operation_plan_publish' || action === 'publish_operation_plan' || action === 'modo_operacao_publish') {
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(publishOperationPlan_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'operation_plan_clear' || action === 'clear_operation_plan' || action === 'modo_operacao_clear' || action === 'operation_day_clear') {
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(clearActiveOperationPlan_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'operation_workday_settings_save' || action === 'operation_jornada_save' || action === 'salvar_jornada_operacao') {
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(saveOperationWorkdaySettings_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'laboratorio_resultado' || action === 'lab_result_update' || action === 'tubito_result_update') {
      assertLaboratoryAccess_(payload);
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(updateLaboratoryTubitoResult_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'supervision_request' || action === 'solicitar_supervisao') {
      ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(payload);
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(saveSupervisionRequest_(payload)));
    }


    if (action === 'supervision_request_status' || action === 'supervision_status' || action === 'atualizar_supervisao_status') {
      assertSupervisionAccess_(payload);
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(updateSupervisionRequestStatus_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'ladder_request_status' || action === 'escada_status' || action === 'atualizar_escada_status') {
      assertSupervisionAccess_(payload);
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(updateLadderRequestStatus_(payload)));
    }

    if (action === 'early_closure_status' || action === 'encerramento_antecipado_status' || action === 'limpar_encerramento_antecipado') {
      assertSupervisionAccess_(payload);
      lock = acquireWriteLock_();
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_(updateEarlyClosureStatus_(payload)));
    }

    if (action !== 'sync') {
      return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_({ ok: false, error: 'Acao nao suportada.', generatedAt: nowIso_() }));
    }

    var operationalSession = ACE_requireOperationalAccess_(payload);
    if (shouldRequireSyncToken_()) {
      requireApiTokenIfConfigured_(payload.token || payload.api_token || payload.auth_token || '');
    }
    lock = acquireWriteLock_();

    var warnings = [];
    var syncScope = ACE_getSyncPermissionScope_(operationalSession);
    var agentRows = validateSyncArray_(payload.agents, 'agents');
    var propertyRows = validateSyncArray_(payload.properties, 'properties');
    var visitRows = validateSyncArray_(payload.visits, 'visits');
    var tubitoRows = validateSyncArray_(payload.tubitos, 'tubitos');
    var supervisionRows = validateSyncArray_(payload.supervision_requests || payload.supervisionRequests, 'supervision_requests');
    var logRows = validateSyncArray_(payload.logs, 'logs');
    var deletedAgents = syncScope.canAdmin ? sanitizeDeletedAgentsPayload_(payload.deleted_agents || payload.deletedAgentRecords || []) : [];
    var metricsDaily = syncScope.canAdmin ? sanitizeMetricsDailyPayload_(payload.metrics_daily) : null;
    var systemState = syncScope.canAdmin ? sanitizeSystemStatePayload_(payload.system_state) : null;

    if (!syncScope.canAdmin && (
      agentRows.length ||
      validateSyncArray_(payload.deleted_agents || payload.deletedAgentRecords || [], 'deleted_agents').length ||
      payload.metrics_daily ||
      payload.system_state
    )) {
      warnings.push('admin_payload_ignored: campos administrativos foram ignorados para sessão de agente.');
    }

    var sanitizedAgents = syncScope.canAdmin ? sanitizeAgentRowsForSync_(agentRows) : [];
    var sanitizedProperties = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(propertyRows, ACE_API.HEADERS.Imoveis);
    var sanitizedVisits = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(visitRows, ACE_API.HEADERS.Visitas);
    var sanitizedTubitos = sanitizeTubitoRowsForSync_(tubitoRows);
    var sanitizedSupervision = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(supervisionRows, ACE_API.HEADERS.SupervisionRequests);
    var sanitizedLogs = normalizeIncomingLogs_(logRows);

    var serverVisitValidation = validateServerVisitRowsForSync_(sanitizedVisits, operationalSession, syncScope);
    sanitizedVisits = serverVisitValidation.valid;
    var serverTubitoValidation = validateServerTubitoRowsForSync_(sanitizedTubitos, serverVisitValidation.invalidVisitIds, operationalSession, syncScope);
    sanitizedTubitos = serverTubitoValidation.valid;

    if (serverVisitValidation.blocked.length) {
      warnings.push('server_validation_visits_blocked: ' + serverVisitValidation.blocked.length);
    }
    if (serverTubitoValidation.blocked.length) {
      warnings.push('server_validation_tubitos_blocked: ' + serverTubitoValidation.blocked.length);
    }

    var propertySyncResult = upsertPropertyRowsWithConflictPolicy_(sanitizedProperties);
    var counts = {
      deleted_agents: removeDeletedAgentsFromSheet_(deletedAgents),
      agents: upsertAgentRowsPreservingSecrets_(sanitizedAgents),
      properties: propertySyncResult.count,
      visits: upsertVisitRowsPreservingLab_(sanitizedVisits),
      tubitos: upsertTubitoRowsPreservingLab_(sanitizedTubitos, sanitizedVisits),
      supervision_requests: upsertRows_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SUPERVISION, sanitizedSupervision, 'uid', ACE_API.HEADERS.SupervisionRequests),
      logs: appendUniqueLogRows_(sanitizedLogs)
    };

    if (metricsDaily) {
      upsertMetricsDaily_(metricsDaily);
      counts.metrics_daily = 1;
    } else {
      counts.metrics_daily = 0;
    }

    var visitStatuses = sanitizedVisits.map(function(row) {
      return { uid: String(row.uid || '').trim(), ok: true, synced: true };
    }).filter(function(row) { return !!row.uid; }).concat(serverVisitValidation.blocked);

    var tubitoStatuses = sanitizedTubitos.map(function(row) {
      return {
        uid: String(row.uid || '').trim(),
        numero_tubito: String(row.numero_tubito || '').trim(),
        ok: true,
        synced: true
      };
    }).filter(function(row) { return !!row.uid || !!row.numero_tubito; }).concat(serverTubitoValidation.blocked);

    var propertyStatuses = propertySyncResult.statuses || [];
    if (propertyStatuses.some(function(row) { return row && row.conflict === true; })) {
      warnings.push('property_conflict: ' + propertyStatuses.filter(function(row) { return row && row.conflict === true; }).length + ' cadastro(s) de imóvel mantidos no aparelho por conflito de versão.');
    }

    var supervisionStatuses = sanitizedSupervision.map(function(row) {
      return { uid: String(row.uid || '').trim(), ok: true, synced: true };
    }).filter(function(row) { return !!row.uid; });

    var syncValidation = payload.sync_validation || {};
    var clientBlockedVisits = Array.isArray(syncValidation.visits_blocked) ? syncValidation.visits_blocked : [];
    var clientBlockedTubitos = Array.isArray(syncValidation.tubitos_blocked) ? syncValidation.tubitos_blocked : [];
    var blockedVisits = clientBlockedVisits.concat(serverVisitValidation.blocked);
    var blockedTubitos = clientBlockedTubitos.concat(serverTubitoValidation.blocked);
    if (blockedVisits.length) {
      warnings.push('sync_validation_visits_blocked: ' + blockedVisits.length);
    }
    if (blockedTubitos.length) {
      warnings.push('sync_validation_tubitos_blocked: ' + blockedTubitos.length);
    }

    try {
      if (systemState) {
        setSystemStateBulk_(systemState);
        counts.system_state = Object.keys(systemState).length;
      } else {
        counts.system_state = 0;
      }
      setSystemState_('last_sync_error', '');
      setSystemState_('last_sync_at', nowIso_());
      appendLogSafe_('sync', 'receive', 'SYNC', 'Sincronizacao concluida com sucesso.');
    } catch (sideErr) {
      warnings.push('sync_side_effects: ' + safeErrorMessage_(sideErr));
    }

    return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_({
      ok: true,
      generatedAt: nowIso_(),
      updatedAt: nowIso_(),
      counts: counts,
      saved: {
        agents: counts.agents || 0,
        deleted_agents: counts.deleted_agents || 0,
        properties: counts.properties || 0,
        visits: counts.visits || 0,
        tubitos: counts.tubitos || 0,
        supervision_requests: counts.supervision_requests || 0,
        logs: counts.logs || 0,
        metrics_daily: counts.metrics_daily || 0,
        system_state: counts.system_state || 0
      },
      processed_ids: {
        visits: visitStatuses.filter(function(row) { return row && row.ok !== false; }).map(function(row) { return row.uid; }),
        tubitos: tubitoStatuses.filter(function(row) { return row && row.ok !== false; }).map(function(row) { return row.uid || row.numero_tubito; }),
        supervision_requests: supervisionStatuses.map(function(row) { return row.uid; }),
        properties: propertyStatuses.map(function(row) { return String(row && row.uid || '').trim(); }).filter(function(uid) { return !!uid; })
      },
      property_statuses: propertyStatuses,
      visit_statuses: visitStatuses,
      tubito_statuses: tubitoStatuses,
      supervision_statuses: supervisionStatuses,
      errors: {
        visits_blocked: blockedVisits,
        tubitos_blocked: blockedTubitos
      },
      warnings: warnings
    }));
  } catch (err) {
    var message = safeErrorMessage_(err);
    try {
      setupProductionSheets();
      setSystemState_('last_sync_error', message);
    } catch (ignore) {}
    return jsonResponse_(wrapPayload_({ ok: false, error: message, generatedAt: nowIso_() }));
  } finally {
    releaseWriteLock_(lock);
  }
}

function buildPublicStatusPayload_() {
  return wrapPayload_({
    ok: true,
    service: ACE_API.APP_NAME,
    version: ACE_API.VERSION,
    generatedAt: nowIso_()
  });
}

function buildStatusPayload_() {
  var spreadsheetInfo = { ok: false, name: '', url: '' };
  try {
    var ss = getTargetSpreadsheet_();
    spreadsheetInfo = { ok: true, name: ss.getName(), url: ss.getUrl() };
  } catch (err) {
    spreadsheetInfo = { ok: false, error: safeErrorMessage_(err) };
  }
  return wrapPayload_({
    ok: true,
    service: ACE_API.APP_NAME,
    version: ACE_API.VERSION,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    actions: ['status', 'status_full', 'bootstrap', 'roster', 'login_bootstrap', 'properties_catalog', 'properties', 'catalogs', 'liraa_plan', 'liraa_plan_publish', 'liraa_plan_clear', 'operation_plan', 'operation_schedule', 'operation_workday_settings', 'operation_workday_settings_save', 'operation_plan_publish', 'operation_plan_clear', 'tubitos', 'laboratorio_tubitos', 'laboratorio_resultado', 'dashboard_range', 'dashboard_summary', 'attendance_summary', 'agent_attendance', 'supervision_requests', 'supervision_request', 'supervision_request_status', 'ladder_request_status', 'backup_export', 'login', 'sync', 'restore_backup', 'reset_operational', 'reset_access', 'reset_clean_base', 'diagnose_active_spreadsheet', 'generate_demo_presentation'],
    counts: getCounts_(),
    spreadsheet: spreadsheetInfo,
    system_state: {
      last_sync_at: getSystemState_('last_sync_at'),
      last_sync_error: getSystemState_('last_sync_error'),
      last_bootstrap_at: getSystemState_('last_bootstrap_at'),
      active_operation_mode: getSystemState_('active_operation_mode'),
      active_operation_released_at: getSystemState_('active_operation_released_at'),
      active_liraa_plan_released_at: getSystemState_('active_liraa_plan_released_at'),
      operation_workday_settings: getOperationWorkdaySettings_()
    }
  });
}

function wrapPayload_(payload) {
  var base = payload || {};
  var data = {};
  Object.keys(base).forEach(function(key) {
    if (['ok', 'error', 'service', 'version', 'generatedAt', 'actions'].indexOf(key) > -1) {
      return;
    }
    data[key] = base[key];
  });
  var wrapped = Object.assign({}, base);
  if (!wrapped.data) {
    wrapped.data = data;
  }
  return wrapped;
}

function buildDashboardMeta_(visits, properties, start, end) {
  var gpsCount = (visits || []).filter(function(row) {
    return !!String(row.gps_lat || '').trim() && !!String(row.gps_lng || '').trim();
  }).length;
  return {
    date_range: { start: start || '', end: end || '' },
    counts: { visits: (visits || []).length, properties: (properties || []).length, gps_visits: gpsCount }
  };
}

function normalizeAreaCode_(value) {
  return String(value || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/\s*\/\s*/g, '/');
}

function parsePositiveInt_(value, fallback, maxValue) {
  var number = Math.floor(Number(value || fallback || 0));
  if (!isFinite(number) || number <= 0) {
    number = Math.floor(Number(fallback || 0)) || 1;
  }
  if (maxValue && number > maxValue) {
    return maxValue;
  }
  return number;
}

function hasFocusInVisitRow_(row) {
  return String(row && row.foco || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'sim' ||
    toNumber_(row && row.focus_count || 0) > 0 ||
    toNumber_(row && row.deposit_focus_count || 0) > 0;
}

function hasGpsInVisitRow_(row) {
  return !!String(row && row.gps_lat || '').trim() && !!String(row && row.gps_lng || '').trim();
}

function parseDepositBreakdownMap_(value) {
  var map = {};
  String(value || '').split(/[|,;]+/).map(function(part) {
    return String(part || '').trim();
  }).filter(Boolean).forEach(function(part) {
    var match = part.match(/^([A-Z0-9]+)\((\d+)\)$/i) || part.match(/^([A-Z0-9]+)\s*[:=-]\s*(\d+)$/i);
    if (!match) {
      return;
    }
    var code = String(match[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    var qty = toNumber_(match[2] || 0);
    if (code && qty > 0) {
      map[code] = (map[code] || 0) + qty;
    }
  });
  return map;
}

function sumDepositBreakdownMap_(map) {
  return Object.keys(map || {}).reduce(function(sum, key) {
    return sum + (toNumber_(map[key] || 0) || 0);
  }, 0);
}

function getVisitBpiGrams_(row) {
  return toNumber_(row && (row.deposit_treatment_count || row.depositTreatmentCount || row.depositTreatmentTotal || row.larvicida_qtd || row.larvicidaQty) || 0);
}

function getVisitTreatedDepositCount_(row) {
  var treatmentMap = parseDepositBreakdownMap_(row && (row.deposit_treatment_breakdown || row.depositTreatmentBreakdown) || '');
  var depositMap = parseDepositBreakdownMap_(row && row.deposits || '');
  var focusMap = parseDepositBreakdownMap_(row && (row.deposit_focus_breakdown || row.depositFocusBreakdown) || '');
  var treated = Object.keys(treatmentMap).reduce(function(sum, code) {
    if (toNumber_(treatmentMap[code] || 0) <= 0) {
      return sum;
    }
    return sum + Math.max(1, toNumber_(depositMap[code] || 0), toNumber_(focusMap[code] || 0));
  }, 0);
  if (treated > 0) {
    return treated;
  }
  if (getVisitBpiGrams_(row) > 0) {
    return sumDepositBreakdownMap_(focusMap) || toNumber_(row && row.deposit_count || 0) || sumDepositBreakdownMap_(depositMap) || 1;
  }
  return 0;
}

function normalizeDashboardSituacaoFilter_(value) {
  var text = String(value || '').trim();
  if (!text) {
    return [];
  }
  if (text === 'Fechado') {
    return ['Fechado', 'Recusa'];
  }
  return [text];
}

function buildDashboardFilters_(params) {
  var start = params.start ? normalizeDateOnly_(params.start) : '';
  var end = params.end ? normalizeDateOnly_(params.end) : '';
  return {
    start: start,
    end: end,
    bairro: normalizeBairroValue_(params.bairro || ''),
    microarea: normalizeMicroareaValue_(params.microarea || '', params.bairro || ''),
    quarteirao: normalizeAreaCode_(params.quarteirao || ''),
    logradouro: normalizeComparableText_(params.logradouro || ''),
    agent: normalizeComparableText_(params.agent || params.agente || ''),
    situacoes: normalizeDashboardSituacaoFilter_(params.situacao || ''),
    foco: String(params.foco || '').trim(),
    gps: String(params.gps || '').trim(),
    operationMode: (params.operation_mode || params.operational_mode || params.operacao) ? sanitizeOperationMode_(params.operation_mode || params.operational_mode || params.operacao) : '',
    search: normalizeComparableText_(params.search || params.q || ''),
    includeProperties: String(params.include_properties || '1') !== '0',
    page: parsePositiveInt_(params.page, 1, 9999),
    pageSize: parsePositiveInt_(params.page_size || params.pageSize, 5000, 10000),
    sort: String(params.sort || 'desc').trim().toLowerCase() === 'asc' ? 'asc' : 'desc'
  };
}

function buildDashboardSearchText_(row) {
  return normalizeComparableText_([
    row && row.bairro,
    row && row.microarea,
    row && row.quarteirao,
    row && row.logradouro,
    row && row.numero,
    row && row.morador,
    row && row.agente,
    row && row.situacao,
    row && row.telefone,
    row && row.operation_mode,
    row && row.pe_tipo_local,
    row && row.pe_nome_local,
    row && row.liraa_ciclo,
    row && row.referencia
  ].join(' '));
}

function visitMatchesDashboardFilters_(row, filters) {
  var date = normalizeDateOnly_(row && row.data || '');
  var bairro = normalizeBairroValue_(row && row.bairro || '');
  var microarea = normalizeMicroareaValue_(row && row.microarea || '', bairro);
  var quarteirao = normalizeAreaCode_(row && row.quarteirao || '');
  var logradouro = normalizeComparableText_(row && row.logradouro || '');
  var agent = normalizeComparableText_(row && (row.agente || row.matricula) || '');
  if (filters.start && (!date || date < filters.start)) { return false; }
  if (filters.end && date && date > filters.end) { return false; }
  if (filters.bairro && bairro !== filters.bairro) { return false; }
  if (filters.microarea && microarea !== filters.microarea) { return false; }
  if (filters.quarteirao && quarteirao !== filters.quarteirao) { return false; }
  if (filters.logradouro && logradouro !== filters.logradouro) { return false; }
  if (filters.agent && agent !== filters.agent) { return false; }
  if (filters.situacoes.length && filters.situacoes.indexOf(String(row && row.situacao || '').trim()) === -1) { return false; }
  if (filters.foco === 'com_foco' && !hasFocusInVisitRow_(row)) { return false; }
  if (filters.foco === 'sem_foco' && hasFocusInVisitRow_(row)) { return false; }
  if (filters.gps === 'com_gps' && !hasGpsInVisitRow_(row)) { return false; }
  if (filters.gps === 'sem_gps' && hasGpsInVisitRow_(row)) { return false; }
  if (filters.operationMode && filters.operationMode !== 'VD' && sanitizeOperationMode_(row && (row.operation_mode || row.operational_mode || row.origem_visita || 'VD')) !== filters.operationMode) { return false; }
  if (filters.operationMode === 'VD' && sanitizeOperationMode_(row && (row.operation_mode || row.operational_mode || row.origem_visita || 'VD')) !== 'VD') { return false; }
  if (filters.search && buildDashboardSearchText_(row).indexOf(filters.search) === -1) { return false; }
  return true;
}

function propertyMatchesDashboardFilters_(row, filters) {
  var bairro = normalizeBairroValue_(row && row.bairro || '');
  var microarea = normalizeMicroareaValue_(row && row.microarea || '', bairro);
  var quarteirao = normalizeAreaCode_(row && row.quarteirao || '');
  var logradouro = normalizeComparableText_(row && row.logradouro || '');
  if (filters.bairro && bairro !== filters.bairro) { return false; }
  if (filters.microarea && microarea !== filters.microarea) { return false; }
  if (filters.quarteirao && quarteirao !== filters.quarteirao) { return false; }
  if (filters.logradouro && logradouro !== filters.logradouro) { return false; }
  if (filters.search && buildDashboardSearchText_(row).indexOf(filters.search) === -1) { return false; }
  return true;
}

function buildPaginationMeta_(totalRows, page, pageSize) {
  var safeTotal = Math.max(0, Number(totalRows || 0));
  var safeSize = parsePositiveInt_(pageSize, 5000, 10000);
  var totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(safeTotal / safeSize));
  var safePage = Math.min(Math.max(1, parsePositiveInt_(page, 1, 9999)), totalPages);
  var startIndex = (safePage - 1) * safeSize;
  var endIndex = Math.min(startIndex + safeSize, safeTotal);
  return {
    page: safePage,
    pageSize: safeSize,
    total: safeTotal,
    totalPages: totalPages,
    startIndex: startIndex,
    endIndex: endIndex,
    returned: Math.max(0, endIndex - startIndex),
    truncated: safeTotal > safeSize
  };
}

function buildDashboardSummary_(visits, properties) {
  var gpsCount = 0;
  var deposits = 0;
  var depositsWithFocus = 0;
  var depositsTreated = 0;
  var bpiGrams = 0;
  var focusVisits = 0;
  var tubitos = 0;
  var opened = 0;
  var closed = 0;
  var recovered = 0;
  var workedPropertyCounts = {};
  var activeAgents = {};
  var focusPerProperty = {};
  (visits || []).forEach(function(row) {
    var propertyKey = String(row && row.property_uid || '').trim() ||
      (normalizeComparableText_(row && row.logradouro || '') + '|' + normalizeComparableText_(row && row.numero || ''));
    var agentKey = String(row && (row.matricula || row.agente) || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (agentKey) {
      activeAgents[agentKey] = true;
    }
    gpsCount += hasGpsInVisitRow_(row) ? 1 : 0;
    deposits += toNumber_(row && row.deposit_count || 0);
    depositsWithFocus += toNumber_(row && row.deposit_focus_count || 0);
    depositsTreated += getVisitTreatedDepositCount_(row);
    bpiGrams += getVisitBpiGrams_(row);
    tubitos += toNumber_(row && row.tubitos_qtd || 0);
    if (hasFocusInVisitRow_(row)) {
      focusVisits += 1;
    }
    if (String(row && row.situacao || '').trim() === 'Visitado') {
      opened += 1;
      if (propertyKey) {
        workedPropertyCounts[propertyKey] = (workedPropertyCounts[propertyKey] || 0) + 1;
      }
    }
    if (String(row && row.situacao || '').trim() === 'Fechado' || String(row && row.situacao || '').trim() === 'Recusa') {
      closed += 1;
    }
    if (String(row && row.situacao || '').trim() === 'Recuperado') {
      recovered += 1;
      if (propertyKey) {
        workedPropertyCounts[propertyKey] = (workedPropertyCounts[propertyKey] || 0) + 1;
      }
    }
    if (propertyKey) {
      if (!focusPerProperty[propertyKey]) {
        focusPerProperty[propertyKey] = { touched: false, hasFocus: false };
      }
      focusPerProperty[propertyKey].touched = true;
      if (hasFocusInVisitRow_(row)) {
        focusPerProperty[propertyKey].hasFocus = true;
      }
    }
  });
  var visitedProperties = opened + recovered;
  var uniqueWorkedProperties = Object.keys(workedPropertyCounts).length;
  var pending = Math.max(0, closed - recovered);
  var focusSummary = Object.keys(focusPerProperty).reduce(function(acc, key) {
    if (focusPerProperty[key].hasFocus) {
      acc.withFocus += 1;
    } else if (focusPerProperty[key].touched) {
      acc.withoutFocus += 1;
    }
    return acc;
  }, { withFocus: 0, withoutFocus: 0 });
  return {
    totalVisits: (visits || []).length,
    activeAgents: Object.keys(activeAgents).length,
    visitedProperties: visitedProperties,
    totalProperties: uniqueWorkedProperties,
    repeatedProperties: Math.max(0, visitedProperties - uniqueWorkedProperties),
    opened: opened,
    closed: closed,
    recovered: recovered,
    pending: pending,
    deposits: deposits,
    depositsWithFocus: depositsWithFocus,
    depositsEliminated: deposits,
    depositsTreated: depositsTreated,
    bpiGrams: bpiGrams,
    tubitos: tubitos,
    focusVisits: focusVisits,
    gpsVisits: gpsCount,
    gpsCoverage: visits && visits.length ? Math.round((gpsCount / visits.length) * 100) : 0,
    infestationRate: deposits ? Number(((depositsWithFocus / deposits) * 100).toFixed(1)) : 0,
    filteredProperties: (properties || []).length,
    propertiesWithFocus: focusSummary.withFocus,
    propertiesWithoutFocus: focusSummary.withoutFocus
  };
}

function buildDashboardFilterCatalogs_(visits, properties) {
  var rows = (visits || []).concat(properties || []);
  return {
    bairros: uniqueSorted_(rows.map(function(row) { return normalizeBairroValue_(row && row.bairro || ''); }), normalizeComparableText_),
    microareas: uniqueSorted_(rows.map(function(row) { return normalizeMicroareaValue_(row && row.microarea || '', row && row.bairro || ''); }), normalizeComparableText_),
    quarteiroes: uniqueSorted_(rows.map(function(row) { return normalizeAreaCode_(row && row.quarteirao || ''); }), normalizeComparableText_),
    logradouros: uniqueSorted_(rows.map(function(row) { return normalizeText_(row && row.logradouro || ''); }), normalizeComparableText_),
    agentes: uniqueSorted_((visits || []).map(function(row) { return normalizeText_(row && row.agente || ''); }), normalizeComparableText_)
  };
}

function buildDashboardResponse_(params, options) {
  var config = options || {};
  var filters = buildDashboardFilters_(params || {});
  var filteredVisits = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS).filter(function(row) {
    return visitMatchesDashboardFilters_(row, filters);
  }).sort(function(a, b) {
    var left = String(a && a.data || '') + ' ' + String(a && a.hora || '');
    var right = String(b && b.data || '') + ' ' + String(b && b.hora || '');
    return filters.sort === 'asc' ? left.localeCompare(right) : right.localeCompare(left);
  });
  var filteredProperties = filters.includeProperties || config.summaryOnly
    ? readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS).filter(function(row) {
        return propertyMatchesDashboardFilters_(row, filters);
      })
    : [];
  var pagination = buildPaginationMeta_(filteredVisits.length, filters.page, filters.pageSize);
  var pagedVisits = config.summaryOnly ? [] : filteredVisits.slice(pagination.startIndex, pagination.endIndex);
  var filteredVisitMap = {};
  filteredVisits.forEach(function(row) {
    var uid = String(row && row.uid || '').trim();
    if (uid) { filteredVisitMap[uid] = true; }
  });
  var filteredTubitos = readLaboratoryTubitoRows_().filter(function(row) {
    var visitUid = String(row && row.visit_uid || '').trim();
    var date = normalizeDateOnly_(row && row.data_coleta || '');
    var operationMode = sanitizeOperationMode_(row && (row.operation_mode || row.origem_visita || 'VD'));
    if (visitUid && filteredVisitMap[visitUid]) { return true; }
    if (filters.start && (!date || date < filters.start)) { return false; }
    if (filters.end && date && date > filters.end) { return false; }
    if (filters.operationMode && operationMode !== filters.operationMode) { return false; }
    return !visitUid && (!filters.bairro || normalizeBairroValue_(row && row.bairro || '') === filters.bairro);
  });
  var summary = buildDashboardSummary_(filteredVisits, filteredProperties);
  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    visits: pagedVisits,
    properties: config.summaryOnly || !filters.includeProperties ? [] : filteredProperties,
    agents: buildPublicAgents_(buildVisibleAgentRoster_()),
    tubitos: buildTubitosForResponse_(filteredTubitos),
    catalogs: {
      bairros: readBairrosCatalog_(),
      filters: buildDashboardFilterCatalogs_(filteredVisits, filteredProperties)
    },
    summary: Object.assign({}, summary, { operationDistribution: buildOperationDistributionSummary_(filters.start || todayDate_(), filteredVisits) }),
    operation_summary: buildOperationDistributionSummary_(filters.start || todayDate_(), filteredVisits),
    meta: Object.assign(buildDashboardMeta_(filteredVisits, filteredProperties, filters.start, filters.end), {
      pagination: {
        visits: {
          page: pagination.page,
          page_size: pagination.pageSize,
          total: pagination.total,
          total_pages: pagination.totalPages,
          returned: pagination.returned,
          truncated: pagination.truncated
        }
      },
      filters_applied: {
        bairro: filters.bairro,
        microarea: filters.microarea,
        quarteirao: filters.quarteirao,
        logradouro: normalizeText_(params.logradouro || ''),
        agent: normalizeText_(params.agent || params.agente || ''),
        situacao: normalizeText_(params.situacao || ''),
        foco: filters.foco,
        gps: filters.gps,
        operation_mode: filters.operationMode,
        search: normalizeText_(params.search || params.q || '')
      }
    })
  };
}

function buildBackupExportPayload_() {
  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    snapshot: {
      version: ACE_API.VERSION,
      exported_at: nowIso_(),
      agents: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES),
      properties: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS),
      visits: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS),
      tubitos: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.TUBITOS),
      operations: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OPERATIONS),
      operation_assignments: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OP_ASSIGNMENTS),
      metrics_daily: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.METRICS),
      logs: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.LOG),
      system_state: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SYSTEM)
    }
  };
}

function restoreBackupPayload_(snapshot) {
  if (!snapshot || typeof snapshot !== 'object' || Array.isArray(snapshot)) {
    throw new Error('Snapshot de backup invalido.');
  }
  var agents = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(snapshot.agents || [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  var properties = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(snapshot.properties || [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Imoveis);
  var visits = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(snapshot.visits || [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Visitas);
  var tubitos = sanitizeTubitoRowsForSync_(snapshot.tubitos || []);
  var operations = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(snapshot.operations || [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Operations);
  var operationAssignments = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(snapshot.operation_assignments || snapshot.operationAssignments || [], ACE_API.HEADERS.OperationAssignments);
  var metrics = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(snapshot.metrics_daily || [], ACE_API.HEADERS.MetricsDaily);
  var logs = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(snapshot.logs || [], ACE_API.HEADERS.Log);
  var systemState = sanitizeRowsForHeaders_(snapshot.system_state || [], ACE_API.HEADERS.SystemState);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.AGENTES, agents, ACE_API.HEADERS.Agentes);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS, properties, ACE_API.HEADERS.Imoveis);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS, visits, ACE_API.HEADERS.Visitas);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.TUBITOS, tubitos, ACE_API.HEADERS.Tubitos);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OPERATIONS, operations, ACE_API.HEADERS.Operations);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OP_ASSIGNMENTS, operationAssignments, ACE_API.HEADERS.OperationAssignments);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.METRICS, metrics, ACE_API.HEADERS.MetricsDaily);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.LOG, logs, ACE_API.HEADERS.Log);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.SYSTEM, systemState, ACE_API.HEADERS.SystemState);
  appendLogSafe_('backup', 'restore', 'BACKUP', 'Snapshot restaurado na planilha operacional.');
  setSystemState_('last_sync_error', '');
  setSystemState_('last_sync_at', nowIso_());
  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    restored: {
      agents: agents.length,
      properties: properties.length,
      visits: visits.length,
      tubitos: tubitos.length,
      operations: operations.length,
      operation_assignments: operationAssignments.length,
      metrics_daily: metrics.length,
      logs: logs.length,
      system_state: systemState.length
    }
  };
}

function getActiveLiraaPlanPayload_() {
  var raw = getSystemState_('active_liraa_plan');
  var releasedAt = getSystemState_('active_liraa_plan_released_at');
  var clearedAt = getSystemState_('active_liraa_plan_cleared_at');
  var plan = null;

  if (raw) {
    try {
      plan = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (err) {
      plan = null;
    }
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    active: !!(plan && plan.active !== false),
    releasedAt: releasedAt || (plan && plan.releasedAt) || '',
    cleared: !plan && !!clearedAt,
    clearedAt: clearedAt || '',
    plan: plan && plan.active !== false ? plan : null
  };
}

function normalizeLiraaTerritoryKey_(value) {
  var key = normalizeComparableText_(stripMicroareaPrefix_(value || ''));
  var aliases = {
    'caixa d agua': 'caixa dagua',
    'caixa dagua': 'caixa dagua',
    'morro do estado': 'ulisses lemgruber',
    'light': 'ilha dos pombos',
    'ilha dos pombos light': 'ilha dos pombos',
    'porto velho': 'porto velho do cunha',
    'porto velho do cunha pvc': 'porto velho do cunha'
  };
  return aliases[key] || key;
}

function isLiraaEligibleTerritory_(value) {
  var allowed = {
    'centro': true,
    'boa ideia': true,
    'botafogo': true,
    'caixa dagua': true,
    'jardim centenario': true,
    'ulisses lemgruber': true,
    'progresso': true,
    'val paraiso': true
  };
  return !!allowed[normalizeLiraaTerritoryKey_(value)];
}

function sanitizeLiraaPlan_(plan) {
  if (!plan || typeof plan !== 'object' || Array.isArray(plan)) {
    throw new Error('Plano LIRAa invalido.');
  }

  var selected = validateSyncArray_(plan.selected, 'selected').slice(0, 2000).map(function(row, index) {
    row = row || {};
    return {
      order: Math.max(1, Number(row.order || index + 1) || index + 1),
      territory: normalizeText_(row.territory || ''),
      quarter: normalizeText_(row.quarter || ''),
      key: normalizeText_(row.key || ''),
      properties: Math.max(0, Number(row.properties || 0) || 0),
      estimatedProperties: Math.max(0, Number(row.estimatedProperties || 0) || 0),
      propertiesToInspect: Math.max(0, Number(row.propertiesToInspect || 0) || 0),
      seedOrder: Math.max(0, Number(row.seedOrder || 0) || 0),
      hasPolygon: !!row.hasPolygon,
      positiveVisits: Math.max(0, Number(row.positiveVisits || 0) || 0),
      positiveDeposits: Math.max(0, Number(row.positiveDeposits || 0) || 0)
    };
  }).filter(function(row) {
    return !!(row.key || row.territory || row.quarter);
  });

  if (!selected.length) {
    throw new Error('Plano LIRAa sem quarteiroes definidos.');
  }

  return {
    version: normalizeText_(plan.version || 'liraa-plan-v1'),
    active: true,
    generatedAt: normalizeText_(plan.generatedAt || nowIso_()),
    releasedAt: nowIso_(),
    releasedBy: normalizeText_(plan.releasedBy || ''),
    cycle: normalizeText_(plan.cycle || 'LIRAa'),
    seed: normalizeText_(plan.seed || ''),
    percent: Math.max(1, Math.min(100, Number(plan.percent || 20) || 20)),
    minBlocks: Math.max(1, Number(plan.minBlocks || 1) || 1),
    method: normalizeText_(plan.method || 'Selecao manual de microareas e quarteiroes para coleta LIRAa.'),
    groups: countLiraaPlanGroups_(selected),
    totalBlocks: selected.length,
    totalProperties: Math.max(0, Number(plan.totalProperties || 0) || 0),
    targetProperties: Math.max(0, Number(plan.targetProperties || 0) || 0),
    manualSelection: plan.manualSelection !== false,
    selectedMap: selected.reduce(function(map, row) { if (row.key) { map[row.key] = true; } return map; }, {}),
    selected: selected
  };
}

function countLiraaPlanGroups_(selected) {
  var groups = {};
  (selected || []).forEach(function(row) {
    var key = normalizeLiraaTerritoryKey_(row.territory || '');
    if (key) {
      groups[key] = true;
    }
  });
  return Object.keys(groups).length;
}

function publishLiraaPlan_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var adminAgent = assertLiraaPlanPublisher_(payload);
  var plan = sanitizeLiraaPlan_(payload.plan || payload.liraa_plan || payload);
  var actor = payload.actor || {};
  var actorLabel = normalizeText_(
    actor.nome ||
    actor.name ||
    actor.matricula ||
    adminAgent.nome ||
    adminAgent.matricula ||
    payload.actor_name ||
    payload.actor_matricula ||
    ''
  );

  plan.releasedAt = nowIso_();
  plan.releasedBy = actorLabel;

  setSystemState_('active_liraa_plan', plan);
  setSystemState_('active_liraa_plan_released_at', plan.releasedAt);
  setSystemState_('active_liraa_plan_cycle', plan.cycle);
  setSystemState_('active_liraa_plan_cleared_at', '');
  appendLogSafe_('liraa', 'publish_plan', 'LIRAA', 'Plano LIRAa liberado: ' + plan.cycle + ' com ' + plan.selected.length + ' quarteirao(s).');

  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    active: true,
    releasedAt: plan.releasedAt,
    plan: plan
  };
}

function clearActiveLiraaPlan_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var clearedAt = nowIso_();
  assertLiraaPlanPublisher_(payload);
  setSystemState_('active_liraa_plan', '');
  setSystemState_('active_liraa_plan_released_at', '');
  setSystemState_('active_liraa_plan_cycle', '');
  setSystemState_('active_liraa_plan_cleared_at', clearedAt);
  appendLogSafe_('liraa', 'clear_plan', 'LIRAA', 'Plano LIRAa ativo removido.');

  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: clearedAt,
    active: false,
    cleared: true,
    clearedAt: clearedAt
  };
}



function parseLiraaCycleJson_(value, fallback) {
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    return value;
  }
  var text = String(value || '').trim();
  if (!text) {
    return fallback || {};
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text);
  } catch (err) {
    return fallback || {};
  }
}

function makeLiraaCycleId_() {
  return 'liraa-cycle-' + todayDate_() + '-' + String(new Date().getTime());
}

function normalizeLiraaCycleArchive_(archive, actor) {
  archive = parseLiraaCycleJson_(archive, {});
  actor = actor || {};
  var summary = parseLiraaCycleJson_(archive.summary, {});
  var plan = parseLiraaCycleJson_(archive.plan, {});
  var now = nowIso_();
  var cycleId = normalizeText_(archive.id || archive.cycle_id || archive.cycleId || '') || makeLiraaCycleId_();
  var archivedAt = normalizeText_(archive.archivedAt || archive.archived_at || now) || now;
  var archivedDate = normalizeDateOnly_(archive.archivedDate || archive.archived_date || archivedAt || todayDate_()) || todayDate_();

  archive.id = cycleId;
  archive.name = normalizeText_(archive.name || archive.nome || plan.cycle || 'LIRAa');
  archive.archivedAt = archivedAt;
  archive.archivedDate = archivedDate;
  archive.summary = summary;
  archive.plan = plan;

  return {
    cycle_id: cycleId,
    nome: archive.name,
    status: 'archived',
    archived_at: archivedAt,
    archived_date: archivedDate,
    archived_by: normalizeText_(actor.nome || actor.name || archive.archivedBy || archive.archived_by || ''),
    archived_by_matricula: normalizeText_(actor.matricula || archive.archivedByMatricula || archive.archived_by_matricula || ''),
    inspected: Math.max(0, Number(summary.inspected || 0) || 0),
    positives: Math.max(0, Number(summary.positives || 0) || 0),
    iip: Number(summary.iip || 0) || 0,
    breteau: Number(summary.breteau || 0) || 0,
    deposits_positive: Math.max(0, Number(summary.depositsPositive || summary.deposits_positive || 0) || 0),
    total_blocks: Math.max(0, Number(summary.totalBlocks || summary.total_blocks || plan.totalBlocks || 0) || 0),
    groups: Math.max(0, Number(summary.groups || plan.groups || 0) || 0),
    target_properties: Math.max(0, Number(summary.targetProperties || summary.target_properties || plan.targetProperties || 0) || 0),
    plan_json: JSON.stringify(plan || {}),
    summary_json: JSON.stringify(summary || {}),
    archive_json: JSON.stringify(archive || {}),
    updatedAt: now
  };
}

function liraaCycleRowToArchive_(row) {
  row = row || {};
  var archive = parseLiraaCycleJson_(row.archive_json || row.archiveJson, null);
  var summary = parseLiraaCycleJson_(row.summary_json || row.summaryJson, {});
  var plan = parseLiraaCycleJson_(row.plan_json || row.planJson, {});

  if (!archive) {
    archive = {
      id: normalizeText_(row.cycle_id || ''),
      name: normalizeText_(row.nome || 'LIRAa'),
      archivedAt: normalizeText_(row.archived_at || row.updatedAt || ''),
      archivedDate: normalizeDateOnly_(row.archived_date || row.archived_at || ''),
      version: 'liraa-cycle-history-cloud',
      summary: summary,
      plan: plan
    };
  }

  archive.id = normalizeText_(archive.id || row.cycle_id || '');
  archive.name = normalizeText_(archive.name || row.nome || 'LIRAa');
  archive.archivedAt = normalizeText_(archive.archivedAt || row.archived_at || '');
  archive.archivedDate = normalizeDateOnly_(archive.archivedDate || row.archived_date || archive.archivedAt || '');
  archive.archivedBy = normalizeText_(archive.archivedBy || row.archived_by || '');
  archive.archivedByMatricula = normalizeText_(archive.archivedByMatricula || row.archived_by_matricula || '');
  archive.summary = archive.summary && typeof archive.summary === 'object' ? archive.summary : summary;
  archive.plan = archive.plan && typeof archive.plan === 'object' ? archive.plan : plan;
  return archive;
}

function readLiraaCycleArchives_() {
  return readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.LIRAA_CYCLES)
    .map(liraaCycleRowToArchive_)
    .filter(function(item) { return !!(item && item.id); })
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      return Date.parse(b.archivedAt || b.archivedDate || '') - Date.parse(a.archivedAt || a.archivedDate || '');
    });
}

function getLiraaCycleHistoryPayload_(params) {
  params = params || {};
  var cycles = readLiraaCycleArchives_();
  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    cycles: cycles,
    liraa_cycles: cycles
  };
}

function archiveLiraaCycle_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var actor = assertLiraaPlanPublisher_(payload);
  var archive = payload.archive || payload.cycle || payload.liraa_cycle || payload.liraaCycle || {};
  var row = normalizeLiraaCycleArchive_(archive, actor);
  upsertRows_(ACE_API.SHEETS.LIRAA_CYCLES, [row], 'cycle_id', ACE_API.HEADERS.LiraaCycles);
  appendLogSafe_('liraa', 'archive_cycle', row.cycle_id, 'Ciclo LIRAa arquivado: ' + row.nome + '.');
  return {
    ok: true,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    saved: true,
    cycle_id: row.cycle_id,
    archive: liraaCycleRowToArchive_(row),
    cycles: readLiraaCycleArchives_(),
    liraa_cycles: readLiraaCycleArchives_()
  };
}


function sanitizeOperationMode_(value) {
  var mode = String(value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if (mode === 'PE' || mode === 'P.E.' || mode === 'PONTO_ESTRATEGICO' || mode === 'PONTO ESTRATEGICO') { return 'PE'; }
  if (mode === 'LIRAA' || mode === 'LIRA') { return 'LIRAA'; }
  return 'VD';
}


function defaultOperationWorkdaySettings_() {
  return {
    VD: { start: '08:00', closeAfter: '15:00', allowEarlyWithJustification: true },
    PE: { start: '08:00', closeAfter: '13:00', allowEarlyWithJustification: true },
    LIRAA: { start: '08:00', closeAfter: '16:00', allowEarlyWithJustification: true }
  };
}

function normalizeOperationTime_(value, fallback) {
  var raw = String(value || '').trim().replace('.', ':');
  var match = raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::?(\d{2}))?$/);
  if (!match) { return fallback || ''; }
  var hour = Math.max(0, Math.min(23, Number(match[1] || 0)));
  var minute = Math.max(0, Math.min(59, Number(match[2] || 0)));
  return String(hour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(minute).padStart(2, '0');
}

function normalizeOperationBool_(value, fallback) {
  if (value === true || value === false) { return value; }
  var raw = String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (['sim', 's', 'true', '1', 'yes'].indexOf(raw) > -1) { return true; }
  if (['nao', 'não', 'n', 'false', '0', 'no'].indexOf(raw) > -1) { return false; }
  return fallback !== false;
}

function sanitizeOperationWorkdaySettings_(settings) {
  var source = settings && typeof settings === 'object' ? settings : {};
  var defaults = defaultOperationWorkdaySettings_();
  var out = {};
  ['VD', 'PE', 'LIRAA'].forEach(function(mode) {
    var raw = source[mode] || source[mode.toLowerCase()] || {};
    var def = defaults[mode];
    out[mode] = {
      start: normalizeOperationTime_(raw.start || raw.inicio || raw.start_at || raw.inicio_previsto, def.start),
      closeAfter: normalizeOperationTime_(raw.closeAfter || raw.close_after || raw.encerrarApos || raw.encerrar_apos || raw.finish_after, def.closeAfter),
      allowEarlyWithJustification: normalizeOperationBool_(raw.allowEarlyWithJustification || raw.allow_early_with_justification || raw.permitir_antecipado_com_justificativa, def.allowEarlyWithJustification)
    };
  });
  return out;
}

function getOperationWorkdaySettings_() {
  var raw = getSystemState_('operation_workday_settings');
  if (raw) {
    try { return sanitizeOperationWorkdaySettings_(JSON.parse(raw)); } catch (ignore) {}
  }
  return defaultOperationWorkdaySettings_();
}

function saveOperationWorkdaySettings_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var adminAgent = assertLiraaPlanPublisher_(payload);
  var settings = sanitizeOperationWorkdaySettings_(payload.workday_settings || payload.workdaySettings || payload.jornada || {});
  setSystemState_('operation_workday_settings', settings);
  setSystemState_('operation_workday_settings_updated_at', nowIso_());
  setSystemState_('operation_workday_settings_updated_by', normalizeText_(adminAgent.nome || adminAgent.matricula || payload.admin_matricula || 'Coordenacao'));
  appendLogSafe_('operacao', 'save_workday_settings', 'JORNADA', 'Jornada por tipo de trabalho atualizada.');
  return { ok: true, generatedAt: nowIso_(), workday_settings: settings };
}

function getOperationWorkdayRuleForMode_(mode) {
  mode = sanitizeOperationMode_(mode || 'VD');
  return getOperationWorkdaySettings_()[mode] || defaultOperationWorkdaySettings_()[mode] || defaultOperationWorkdaySettings_().VD;
}

function operationModeLabel_(mode) {
  mode = sanitizeOperationMode_(mode);
  if (mode === 'PE') { return 'P.E. - Ponto estrategico'; }
  if (mode === 'LIRAA') { return 'LIRAa'; }
  return 'VD - Visita domiciliar';
}

function todayDate_() {
  return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone() || 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
}

function operationDateFromPayload_(payload) {
  return normalizeDateOnly_(payload && (payload.data_operacao || payload.date || payload.data || payload.reference_date)) || todayDate_();
}

function buildOperationId_(date, mode) {
  var suffix = Utilities.getUuid().slice(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  return 'OP-' + String(date || todayDate_()).replace(/-/g, '') + '-' + sanitizeOperationMode_(mode) + '-' + suffix;
}

function splitOperationTerritoryList_(value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return value.map(function(item) { return normalizeText_(item); }).filter(Boolean);
  }
  return String(value || '')
    .split(/[;,|\n]/)
    .map(function(item) { return normalizeText_(item); })
    .filter(Boolean);
}

function normalizeOperationUnitQuarter_(value) {
  return normalizeText_(value || '')
    .replace(/^Q\s*/i, '')
    .replace(/^0+(\d)/, '$1');
}

function normalizeOperationUnitRow_(row) {
  row = row || {};
  var microarea = normalizeMicroareaValue_(row.microarea || row.territory || row.area || row.bairro || '', '');
  var quarteirao = normalizeOperationUnitQuarter_(row.quarteirao || row.quarter || row.block || '');
  if (!microarea || !quarteirao) {
    return null;
  }
  return {
    key: normalizeText_(row.key || (normalizeComparableText_(microarea) + '|' + normalizeComparableText_(quarteirao))),
    microarea: microarea,
    quarteirao: quarteirao
  };
}

function parseOperationUnitsForStorage_(value) {
  var rows = [];
  var seen = {};
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    rows = value;
  } else if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    rows = [value];
  } else {
    var textValue = normalizeText_(value || '');
    if (!textValue) {
      return [];
    }
    try {
      rows = JSON.parse(textValue);
    } catch (err) {
      return [];
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(rows)) {
      rows = [rows];
    }
  }
  return rows.map(normalizeOperationUnitRow_).filter(function(row) {
    if (!row || seen[row.key]) {
      return false;
    }
    seen[row.key] = true;
    return true;
  });
}

function normalizeOperationUnitsForStorage_(value) {
  var units = parseOperationUnitsForStorage_(value);
  return units.length ? JSON.stringify(units) : '';
}

function requireOperationUnitsForPlan_(plan) {
  var units = parseOperationUnitsForStorage_(plan && plan.operation_units);
  if (!units.length) {
    throw new Error('Selecione ao menos um par microarea + quarteirao para publicar a operacao.');
  }
  plan.operation_units = JSON.stringify(units);
  plan.operation_microareas = units.map(function(row) { return row.microarea; }).filter(function(item, index, arr) {
    return !!item && arr.indexOf(item) === index;
  });
  plan.operation_quarteiroes = units.map(function(row) { return row.quarteirao; }).filter(function(item, index, arr) {
    return !!item && arr.indexOf(item) === index;
  });
  plan.territory_lock = 'Sim';
  return units;
}


function sanitizeOperationPlan_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var plan = payload.plan || payload.operation_plan || payload.modo_operacao || payload.operation || payload;
  var rawAgents = plan.agents || plan.agent_matriculas || plan.agentes || payload.agents || [];
  if (!Array.isArray(rawAgents)) {
    rawAgents = String(rawAgents || '').split(/[;,|\n]/);
  }
  var agentMatriculas = rawAgents.map(function(item) {
    if (item && typeof item === 'object') {
      return String(item.matricula || item.code || item.uid || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    }
    return String(item || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  }).filter(function(item, index, arr) {
    return !!item && arr.indexOf(item) === index;
  });
  var mode = sanitizeOperationMode_(plan.mode || plan.operation_mode || plan.tipo || plan.type || payload.mode || payload.tipo_operacao);
  var pe = plan.pe || payload.pe || {};
  var date = operationDateFromPayload_(plan.data_operacao ? plan : payload);
  return {
    operation_id: normalizeText_(plan.operation_id || plan.id || payload.operation_id || ''),
    version: normalizeText_(plan.version || 'operation-daily-v1'),
    data_operacao: date,
    date: date,
    mode: mode,
    tipo_operacao: mode,
    label: operationModeLabel_(mode),
    title: normalizeText_(plan.title || plan.titulo || payload.title || payload.titulo || ''),
    titulo: normalizeText_(plan.title || plan.titulo || payload.title || payload.titulo || ''),
    cycle: normalizeText_(plan.cycle || plan.ciclo || payload.cycle || ''),
    ciclo: normalizeText_(plan.cycle || plan.ciclo || payload.cycle || ''),
    description: normalizeText_(plan.description || plan.descricao || payload.description || ''),
    descricao: normalizeText_(plan.description || plan.descricao || payload.description || ''),
    operation_microareas: splitOperationTerritoryList_(plan.operation_microareas || plan.microareas || plan.vd_microareas || payload.operation_microareas || payload.microareas || ''),
    operation_quarteiroes: splitOperationTerritoryList_(plan.operation_quarteiroes || plan.quarteiroes || plan.vd_quarteiroes || payload.operation_quarteiroes || payload.quarteiroes || ''),
    operation_units: normalizeOperationUnitsForStorage_(plan.operation_units || plan.operationUnits || plan.territory_units || plan.territoryUnits || payload.operation_units || payload.operationUnits || ''),
    territory_lock: String(plan.territory_lock == null ? (payload.territory_lock == null ? 'Sim' : payload.territory_lock) : plan.territory_lock).trim(),
    agents: agentMatriculas,
    pe: {
      tipo_local: normalizeText_(pe.tipo_local || pe.tipoLocal || plan.pe_tipo_local || payload.pe_tipo_local || ''),
      nome_local: normalizeText_(pe.nome_local || pe.nomeLocal || plan.pe_nome_local || payload.pe_nome_local || ''),
      observacao: normalizeText_(pe.observacao || pe.obs || plan.pe_observacao || payload.pe_observacao || '')
    },
    liraa_plan_id: normalizeText_(plan.liraa_plan_id || plan.liraaPlanoId || payload.liraa_plan_id || ''),
    workday_settings: getOperationWorkdaySettings_(),
    workday_rule: getOperationWorkdayRuleForMode_(mode),
    status: normalizeText_(plan.status || payload.status || 'Ativa') || 'Ativa',
    releasedAt: normalizeText_(plan.releasedAt || plan.released_at || plan.created_at || ''),
    releasedBy: normalizeText_(plan.releasedBy || plan.released_by || plan.created_by || '')
  };
}

function buildAgentRosterMap_() {
  var map = {};
  buildVisibleAgentRoster_().forEach(function(agent) {
    var mat = String(agent.matricula || agent.uid || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (mat) { map[mat] = agent; }
  });
  return map;
}

function isActiveOperationStatus_(status) {
  var value = String(status || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  return !value || value === 'ativa' || value === 'ativo' || value === 'active' || value === 'liberada' || value === 'liberado';
}

function readOperationRowsForDate_(date) {
  date = normalizeDateOnly_(date) || todayDate_();
  return readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OPERATIONS).filter(function(row) {
    return normalizeDateOnly_(row.data_operacao || row.date || '') === date && isActiveOperationStatus_(row.status);
  });
}

function readAssignmentRowsForDate_(date) {
  date = normalizeDateOnly_(date) || todayDate_();
  return readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OP_ASSIGNMENTS).filter(function(row) {
    return normalizeDateOnly_(row.data_operacao || row.date || '') === date && isActiveOperationStatus_(row.status);
  });
}

function operationRowToPlan_(row, assignments) {
  assignments = assignments || [];
  var mode = sanitizeOperationMode_(row.tipo_operacao || row.mode || 'VD');
  var agents = assignments.filter(function(a) {
    return String(a.operation_id || '').trim() === String(row.operation_id || '').trim();
  }).map(function(a) { return String(a.agente_matricula || '').trim().toUpperCase(); }).filter(Boolean);
  var plan = sanitizeOperationPlan_({
    operation_id: row.operation_id,
    data_operacao: row.data_operacao,
    mode: mode,
    titulo: row.titulo,
    ciclo: row.ciclo,
    descricao: row.descricao,
    agents: agents,
    pe_tipo_local: row.pe_tipo_local,
    pe_nome_local: row.pe_nome_local,
    operation_microareas: row.operation_microareas,
    operation_quarteiroes: row.operation_quarteiroes,
    operation_units: row.operation_units,
    territory_lock: row.territory_lock,
    liraa_plan_id: row.liraa_plan_id,
    status: row.status,
    releasedAt: row.created_at,
    releasedBy: row.created_by
  });
  plan.operation_id = String(row.operation_id || '').trim();
  plan.assignments = assignments.filter(function(a) { return String(a.operation_id || '').trim() === plan.operation_id; });
  return plan;
}

function buildOperationScheduleSummary_(operations, assignments, visits, tubitoRows, date) {
  var byMode = {
    VD: { agents: 0, visits: 0, focos: 0, tubitos: 0, pendencias: 0 },
    PE: { agents: 0, visits: 0, focos: 0, tubitos: 0, pendencias: 0 },
    LIRAA: { agents: 0, visits: 0, focos: 0, tubitos: 0, pendencias: 0 }
  };
  var visitModeByUid = {};
  var tubitosByVisitUid = {};
  var tubitoKeys = {};
  var summaryDate = normalizeDateOnly_(date || (operations && operations[0] && operations[0].data_operacao) || (assignments && assignments[0] && assignments[0].data_operacao) || (visits && visits[0] && visits[0].data) || '') || todayDate_();

  (assignments || []).forEach(function(row) {
    var mode = sanitizeOperationMode_(row.tipo_operacao || 'VD');
    byMode[mode].agents += 1;
  });

  (visits || []).forEach(function(row) {
    var mode = sanitizeOperationMode_(row.operation_mode || row.operational_mode || row.origem_visita || 'VD');
    var uid = String(row.uid || '').trim();
    if (uid) { visitModeByUid[uid] = mode; }
    byMode[mode].visits += 1;
    byMode[mode].focos += toNumber_(row.focus_count || row.deposit_focus_count || 0);
    var flags = String(row.quality_flags || row.quality_status || '').toLowerCase();
    var gpsMissing = !(row.gps_lat && row.gps_lng);
    if (flags.indexOf('pend') > -1 || flags.indexOf('gps') > -1 || gpsMissing) {
      byMode[mode].pendencias += 1;
    }
  });

  sanitizeTubitoRowsForSync_(tubitoRows || readLaboratoryTubitoRows_()).forEach(function(row) {
    var uid = String(row.visit_uid || '').trim();
    var rowDate = normalizeDateOnly_(row.data_coleta || row.data || '');
    var belongsToDayVisit = uid && visitModeByUid[uid];
    var key = String(row.uid || row.numero_tubito || '').trim();
    var mode;
    if (!belongsToDayVisit && rowDate && rowDate !== summaryDate) { return; }
    if (key && tubitoKeys[key]) { return; }
    if (key) { tubitoKeys[key] = true; }
    mode = sanitizeOperationMode_(visitModeByUid[uid] || row.operation_mode || row.operational_mode || row.origem_visita || 'VD');
    byMode[mode].tubitos += 1;
    if (uid) { tubitosByVisitUid[uid] = (tubitosByVisitUid[uid] || 0) + 1; }
  });

  (visits || []).forEach(function(row) {
    var uid = String(row.uid || '').trim();
    var qty = toNumber_(row.tubitos_qtd || 0);
    var mode = sanitizeOperationMode_(row.operation_mode || row.operational_mode || row.origem_visita || 'VD');
    if (qty > 0 && (!uid || !tubitosByVisitUid[uid])) {
      byMode[mode].tubitos += qty;
    }
  });

  return {
    total_operations: (operations || []).length,
    total_assignments: (assignments || []).length,
    modes: byMode,
    vd: byMode.VD,
    pe: byMode.PE,
    liraa: byMode.LIRAA
  };
}

function buildOperationDistributionSummary_(date, visits) {
  date = normalizeDateOnly_(date) || todayDate_();
  var ops = readOperationRowsForDate_(date);
  var assigns = readAssignmentRowsForDate_(date);
  var dayVisits = visits || readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS).filter(function(row) {
    return normalizeDateOnly_(row.data || '') === date;
  });
  return buildOperationScheduleSummary_(ops, assigns, dayVisits, readLaboratoryTubitoRows_(), date);
}

function getDailyOperationSchedulePayload_(params) {
  params = params || {};
  var date = operationDateFromPayload_(params);
  var assignments = readAssignmentRowsForDate_(date);
  var operations = readOperationRowsForDate_(date).map(function(row) { return operationRowToPlan_(row, assignments); });
  var assignmentByAgent = {};
  assignments.forEach(function(row) {
    var mat = String(row.agente_matricula || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (mat && !assignmentByAgent[mat]) { assignmentByAgent[mat] = row; }
  });
  var matricula = String(params.matricula || params.agent_matricula || params.agent || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  var agentAssignment = matricula ? assignmentByAgent[matricula] || null : null;
  var agentPlan = null;
  if (agentAssignment) {
    agentPlan = operations.filter(function(op) { return op.operation_id === String(agentAssignment.operation_id || '').trim(); })[0] || null;
  }
  var visits = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS).filter(function(row) { return normalizeDateOnly_(row.data || '') === date; });
  return {
    ok: true,
    active: !!operations.length,
    generatedAt: nowIso_(),
    date: date,
    operations: operations,
    assignments: assignments,
    assignment_by_agent: assignmentByAgent,
    agent_assignment: agentAssignment,
    plan: agentPlan,
    applies_to_agent: !!agentPlan,
    workday_settings: getOperationWorkdaySettings_(),
    summary: buildOperationScheduleSummary_(operations, assignments, visits, readLaboratoryTubitoRows_(), date)
  };
}

function getActiveOperationPlanPayload_(params) {
  params = params || {};
  var schedule = getDailyOperationSchedulePayload_(params);
  if (schedule.plan) {
    return {
      ok: true,
      active: true,
      generatedAt: nowIso_(),
      date: schedule.date,
      applies_to_agent: true,
      plan: schedule.plan,
      operation: schedule.plan,
      assignment: schedule.agent_assignment,
      workday_settings: schedule.workday_settings || getOperationWorkdaySettings_(),
      workday_rule: getOperationWorkdayRuleForMode_(schedule.plan.mode),
      schedule: schedule
    };
  }
  if (params && (params.matricula || params.agent_matricula || params.agent)) {
    return {
      ok: true,
      active: false,
      generatedAt: nowIso_(),
      date: schedule.date,
      applies_to_agent: false,
      plan: sanitizeOperationPlan_({ mode: 'VD', data_operacao: schedule.date, agents: [] }),
      operation: null,
      assignment: null,
      workday_settings: schedule.workday_settings || getOperationWorkdaySettings_(),
      workday_rule: getOperationWorkdayRuleForMode_('VD'),
      schedule: schedule
    };
  }
  return { ok: true, active: !!schedule.operations.length, generatedAt: nowIso_(), date: schedule.date, plan: null, workday_settings: schedule.workday_settings || getOperationWorkdaySettings_(), schedule: schedule, summary: schedule.summary };
}

function publishOperationPlan_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var adminAgent = assertLiraaPlanPublisher_(payload);
  var plan = sanitizeOperationPlan_(payload.plan || payload.operation_plan || payload);
  var date = plan.data_operacao || todayDate_();
  var roster = buildAgentRosterMap_();
  var unknown = plan.agents.filter(function(mat) { return !roster[mat]; });
  if (unknown.length) {
    throw new Error('Agente nao encontrado: ' + unknown.join(', '));
  }
  if (!plan.agents.length) {
    throw new Error('Selecione ao menos um agente para esta operacao.');
  }
  requireOperationUnitsForPlan_(plan);
  var allOps = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OPERATIONS);
  var allAssignments = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OP_ASSIGNMENTS);
  var activeAssignments = allAssignments.filter(function(row) {
    return normalizeDateOnly_(row.data_operacao || '') === date && isActiveOperationStatus_(row.status);
  });
  var conflicts = [];
  var activeByAgent = {};
  activeAssignments.forEach(function(row) {
    var mat = String(row.agente_matricula || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (mat) { activeByAgent[mat] = row; }
  });
  plan.agents.forEach(function(mat) {
    if (activeByAgent[mat]) { conflicts.push(mat + ' (' + sanitizeOperationMode_(activeByAgent[mat].tipo_operacao) + ')'); }
  });
  if (conflicts.length) {
    throw new Error('Agente ja alocado hoje: ' + conflicts.join(', ') + '. Remova/limpe a operacao antes de realocar.');
  }
  var now = nowIso_();
  var opId = plan.operation_id || buildOperationId_(date, plan.mode);
  var opRow = sanitizeObjectForHeaders_({
    operation_id: opId,
    data_operacao: date,
    tipo_operacao: plan.mode,
    titulo: plan.titulo || plan.title || operationModeLabel_(plan.mode),
    ciclo: plan.ciclo || plan.cycle || '',
    descricao: plan.descricao || plan.description || '',
    status: 'Ativa',
    pe_tipo_local: plan.pe.tipo_local,
    pe_nome_local: plan.pe.nome_local,
    operation_microareas: (plan.operation_microareas || []).join('|'),
    operation_quarteiroes: (plan.operation_quarteiroes || []).join('|'),
    operation_units: plan.operation_units || '',
    territory_lock: plan.territory_lock || 'Sim',
    liraa_plan_id: plan.liraa_plan_id,
    created_at: now,
    created_by: normalizeText_(adminAgent.nome || adminAgent.matricula || payload.actor_name || 'Coordenacao'),
    created_by_matricula: normalizeText_(adminAgent.matricula || payload.admin_matricula || ''),
    updatedAt: now
  }, ACE_API.HEADERS.Operations);
  var assignmentRows = plan.agents.map(function(mat) {
    var agent = roster[mat] || {};
    return sanitizeObjectForHeaders_({
      assignment_id: opId + '-' + mat,
      operation_id: opId,
      data_operacao: date,
      agente_matricula: mat,
      agente_nome: normalizeText_(agent.nome || mat),
      tipo_operacao: plan.mode,
      status: 'Ativa',
      assigned_at: now,
      assigned_by: opRow.created_by,
      assigned_by_matricula: opRow.created_by_matricula,
      updatedAt: now
    }, ACE_API.HEADERS.OperationAssignments);
  });
  allOps.push(opRow);
  allAssignments = allAssignments.concat(assignmentRows);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OPERATIONS, allOps, ACE_API.HEADERS.Operations);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OP_ASSIGNMENTS, allAssignments, ACE_API.HEADERS.OperationAssignments);
  setSystemState_('active_operation_mode', 'MULTI');
  setSystemState_('active_operation_released_at', now);
  appendLogSafe_('operacao', 'publish_plan', opId, 'Operacao diaria criada: ' + plan.mode + ' para ' + plan.agents.length + ' agente(s).');
  var schedule = getDailyOperationSchedulePayload_({ date: date });
  return { ok: true, generatedAt: nowIso_(), active: true, plan: operationRowToPlan_(opRow, assignmentRows), operation: operationRowToPlan_(opRow, assignmentRows), schedule: schedule, operations: schedule.operations, assignments: schedule.assignments, summary: schedule.summary };
}

function clearActiveOperationPlan_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var adminAgent = assertLiraaPlanPublisher_(payload);
  var date = operationDateFromPayload_(payload);
  var operationId = normalizeText_(payload.operation_id || payload.id || '');
  var ops = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OPERATIONS);
  var assigns = readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OP_ASSIGNMENTS);
  var canceledOps = 0;
  var canceledAssigns = 0;
  var now = nowIso_();
  ops = ops.map(function(row) {
    var matchDate = normalizeDateOnly_(row.data_operacao || '') === date;
    var matchId = operationId ? String(row.operation_id || '').trim() === operationId : matchDate;
    if (matchId && isActiveOperationStatus_(row.status)) {
      row.status = 'Cancelada';
      row.updatedAt = now;
      canceledOps += 1;
    }
    return sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(row, ACE_API.HEADERS.Operations);
  });
  assigns = assigns.map(function(row) {
    var matchDate = normalizeDateOnly_(row.data_operacao || '') === date;
    var matchId = operationId ? String(row.operation_id || '').trim() === operationId : matchDate;
    if (matchId && isActiveOperationStatus_(row.status)) {
      row.status = 'Cancelada';
      row.updatedAt = now;
      canceledAssigns += 1;
    }
    return sanitizeObjectForHeaders_(row, ACE_API.HEADERS.OperationAssignments);
  });
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OPERATIONS, ops, ACE_API.HEADERS.Operations);
  writeObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OP_ASSIGNMENTS, assigns, ACE_API.HEADERS.OperationAssignments);
  appendLogSafe_('operacao', 'clear_plan', operationId || date, 'Programacao operacional cancelada por ' + normalizeText_(adminAgent.nome || adminAgent.matricula || 'Coordenacao'));
  var schedule = getDailyOperationSchedulePayload_({ date: date });
  if (!schedule.operations.length) {
    setSystemState_('active_operation_mode', '');
    setSystemState_('active_operation_released_at', '');
  }
  return { ok: true, generatedAt: nowIso_(), active: !!schedule.operations.length, cleared: true, canceled: true, canceled_operations: canceledOps, canceled_assignments: canceledAssigns, removed_operations: canceledOps, removed_assignments: canceledAssigns, schedule: schedule, operations: schedule.operations, assignments: schedule.assignments, summary: schedule.summary };
}

function getConfiguredLabAccessKey_() {
  return '';
}

function ACE_allowLegacyPanelHashAccess_() {
  var value = String(ACE_getScriptProperty_('ACE_ALLOW_LEGACY_PANEL_HASH_AUTH', '0') || '0').trim().toLowerCase();
  return value === '1' || value === 'true' || value === 'yes' || value === 'on';
}

function assertLaboratoryAccess_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var sessionToken = payload.sessionToken || payload.session_token || payload.token || payload.auth_token || '';
  if (sessionToken) {
    return ACE_panelRequirePrivateSession_(Object.assign({}, payload, {
      access_module: 'laboratorio',
      accessModule: 'laboratorio'
    }));
  }

  if (!ACE_allowLegacyPanelHashAccess_()) {
    throw new Error('Acesso do laboratório restrito à sessão privada do painel. Entre com CPF e senha do administrador.');
  }

  var admin = payload.admin || {};
  var auth = authenticatePanelAdmin_({
    cpf: payload.admin_cpf || payload.adminCpf || payload.cpf || admin.cpf || admin.login_cpf || '',
    matricula: payload.admin_matricula || admin.matricula || admin.login || admin.username || '',
    senhaHash: payload.admin_hash || payload.adminHash || payload.senhaHash || payload.password_hash || admin.senhaHash || admin.password_hash || admin.hash || '',
    allowHash: true
  });

  if (auth && auth.ok === true && auth.admin === true) {
    return auth.agent || true;
  }

  throw new Error((auth && auth.error) || 'Acesso do laboratorio restrito a administrador com CPF e senha.');
}

function assertSupervisionAccess_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var request = Object.assign({}, payload, {
    access_module: 'supervisao',
    accessModule: 'supervisao'
  });
  return ACE_panelRequirePrivateSession_(request);
}


function assertLiraaPlanPublisher_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var sessionToken = payload.sessionToken || payload.session_token || payload.token || payload.auth_token || '';
  if (sessionToken) {
    return ACE_panelRequirePrivateSession_(Object.assign({}, payload, {
      access_module: 'coordenacao',
      accessModule: 'coordenacao'
    }));
  }

  if (!ACE_allowLegacyPanelHashAccess_()) {
    throw new Error('Sessão privada do painel obrigatória para liberar o plano LIRAa.');
  }

  var admin = payload.admin || {};
  var auth = authenticatePanelAdmin_({
    matricula: payload.admin_matricula || admin.matricula || admin.login || admin.username || ACE_API.ADMIN_MATRICULA,
    senhaHash: payload.admin_hash || payload.senhaHash || payload.password_hash || admin.senhaHash || admin.password_hash || admin.hash || '',
    allowHash: true
  });

  if (!auth || auth.ok !== true || auth.admin !== true || !auth.agent) {
    throw new Error((auth && auth.error) || 'Senha administrativa obrigatoria para liberar o plano LIRAa.');
  }

  return auth.agent;
}

function getCounts_() {
  return {
    agentes: readAgentRowsRobust_().filter(function(row) { return !isRemovedAgentRow_(row); }).length,
    imoveis: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.IMOVEIS).length,
    visitas: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.VISITAS).length,
    tubitos: readLaboratoryTubitoRows_().length,
    operations: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OPERATIONS).length,
    operation_assignments: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.OP_ASSIGNMENTS).length,
    metrics_daily: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.METRICS).length,
    log: readRowsAsObjects_(ACE_API.SHEETS.LOG).length
  };
}

function sanitizeJsonpCallback_(callback) {
  var value = String(callback || '').trim();
  if (!value) { return ''; }
  return /^[A-Za-z_$][0-9A-Za-z_$]*(\.[A-Za-z_$][0-9A-Za-z_$]*)*$/.test(value) ? value : '';
}

function jsonResponse_(payload) {
  return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(payload)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}

function respond_(callback, payload) {
  var safeCallback = sanitizeJsonpCallback_(callback);
  if (safeCallback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(safeCallback + '(' + JSON.stringify(payload) + ');').setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return jsonResponse_(payload);
}

function parseJsonBody_(e) {
  var contents = e && e.postData && typeof e.postData.contents === 'string' ? e.postData.contents : '';
  if (!contents) { throw new Error('Body JSON ausente.'); }
  try {
    var payload = JSON.parse(contents);
    if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object' || Array.isArray(payload)) {
      throw new Error('Payload deve ser um objeto JSON.');
    }
    return payload;
  } catch (err) {
    throw new Error(err && err.message === 'Payload deve ser um objeto JSON.' ? err.message : 'JSON invalido no body.');
  }
}

function validateSyncArray_(rows, fieldName) {
  if (!rows) { return []; }
  if (!Array.isArray(rows)) { throw new Error(fieldName + ' deve ser um array.'); }
  return rows;
}

function ACE_syncPushRequiredError_(errors, condition, message) {
  if (condition) { errors.push(message); }
}

function ACE_syncRowIdentity_(row) {
  row = row || {};
  return {
    uid: String(row.uid || '').trim(),
    numero_tubito: String(row.numero_tubito || row.numeroTubito || '').trim(),
    visit_uid: String(row.visit_uid || row.visitUid || '').trim()
  };
}

function ACE_buildSyncBlockedStatus_(row, errors) {
  var identity = ACE_syncRowIdentity_(row);
  return {
    uid: identity.uid,
    numero_tubito: identity.numero_tubito,
    visit_uid: identity.visit_uid,
    ok: false,
    synced: false,
    errors: (errors || []).slice()
  };
}

function ACE_syncShouldEnforceSession_(session, scope) {
  return !!session && !(scope && scope.canAdmin);
}

function ACE_syncSessionMatricula_(session) {
  return String(session && (session.matricula || session.agent_matricula || '') || '').trim().toUpperCase();
}

function ACE_syncSessionName_(session) {
  return normalizeText_(session && (session.nome || session.agent_name || '') || '');
}

function ACE_syncOperationUnitKey_(microarea, quarteirao) {
  var unit = normalizeOperationUnitRow_({
    microarea: microarea || '',
    quarteirao: quarteirao || ''
  });
  return unit ? unit.key : '';
}

function ACE_syncFindActivePlanForVisit_(row, matricula) {
  var date = normalizeDateOnly_(row && row.data || '');
  var mode = sanitizeOperationMode_(row && (row.operation_mode || row.operational_mode || row.origem_visita || 'VD'));
  var assignments;
  var operations;
  var foundAssignment = null;
  var foundOperation = null;

  if (!date || !matricula) {
    return null;
  }

  assignments = readAssignmentRowsForDate_(date).filter(function(assignment) {
    return String(assignment.agente_matricula || '').trim().toUpperCase() === matricula &&
      sanitizeOperationMode_(assignment.tipo_operacao || mode) === mode;
  });
  operations = readOperationRowsForDate_(date);

  assignments.some(function(assignment) {
    var operationId = String(assignment.operation_id || '').trim();
    foundOperation = operations.filter(function(operation) {
      return String(operation.operation_id || '').trim() === operationId &&
        sanitizeOperationMode_(operation.tipo_operacao || operation.mode || 'VD') === mode;
    })[0] || null;
    if (foundOperation) {
      foundAssignment = assignment;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  });

  if (!foundOperation) {
    return null;
  }

  return operationRowToPlan_(foundOperation, [foundAssignment]);
}

function ACE_syncValidateVisitOperationScope_(row, matricula, errors) {
  var plan = ACE_syncFindActivePlanForVisit_(row, matricula);
  var allowedUnits;
  var declaredUnits;
  var allowedKeys = {};
  var visitUnitKey;

  if (!plan) {
    ACE_syncPushRequiredError_(errors, true, 'Visita sem operacao ativa para agente, data e tipo informado');
    return;
  }

  allowedUnits = parseOperationUnitsForStorage_(plan.operation_units || plan.operationUnits || '');
  if (!allowedUnits.length) {
    ACE_syncPushRequiredError_(errors, true, 'Operacao ativa sem pares microarea + quarteirao');
    return;
  }

  allowedUnits.forEach(function(unit) {
    allowedKeys[unit.key] = true;
  });

  visitUnitKey = ACE_syncOperationUnitKey_(
    row.microarea || row.operation_microarea_designada || row.bairro || '',
    row.quarteirao || row.operation_quarteirao_designado || ''
  );

  ACE_syncPushRequiredError_(errors, !visitUnitKey, 'Visita sem par microarea + quarteirao');
  if (visitUnitKey && !allowedKeys[visitUnitKey]) {
    ACE_syncPushRequiredError_(errors, true, 'Imovel fora dos pares microarea + quarteirao da operacao ativa');
  }

  declaredUnits = parseOperationUnitsForStorage_(row.operation_units_designadas || row.operationUnits || row.operation_units || '');
  ACE_syncPushRequiredError_(errors, !declaredUnits.length, 'Visita sem operation_units_designadas');

  declaredUnits.forEach(function(unit) {
    if (!allowedKeys[unit.key]) {
      ACE_syncPushRequiredError_(errors, true, 'operation_units_designadas contem par fora da operacao ativa');
    }
  });

  if (visitUnitKey && declaredUnits.length && !declaredUnits.some(function(unit) { return unit.key === visitUnitKey; })) {
